Conclusion: Koala Gripper在提升灵巧性和抓取能力的同时保留了可扩展性和易用性，为数据采集与机器人执行设备的一体化设计提供了有效方案。

Abstract: As the demand for larger manipulation datasets grows, handheld robotic gripper data collection and the associated gripper designs become more vital. Current data collection device designs trend towards matching the morphologies of existing robotic grippers, sacrificing ergonomics and manipulation performance. In this paper, we propose a co-design framework that guides the simultaneous development of both data collection and robotic execution devices by weaving both platform constraints into the design process. Through this workflow, we present the Koala Gripper system, a data capture device and robotic gripper platform that improves dexterity and grasp capability compared to parallel jaw grippers while preserving scalability and ease-of-use. The design introduces a novel force-optimized finger/trigger linkage mechanism with directional reflected mass characteristics, a unique monolithic dual-thumb, and user-centered ergonomic design. The design's actuated robotic fingers are backdrivable, with effective mass on the order of tens of grams. We show that these grippers are capable of secure grasps over a wide range of objects, forceful tool use, and precise singulation. We further validate the platform by deploying it with an end-to-end data collection and policy execution pipeline that highlights its capabilities through learning from demonstration. More information available at http://koalagripper.rai-inst.com

</details>


### [156] [Logic-VLA: A Temporal Logic Conditioned Vision-Language-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20556)
*Celina Shiyu Wang,Yiqi Zhao,Junjie Ye,Yue Wang,Jyotirmoy V. Deshmukh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Logic-VLA，一种形式化需求感知的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型，通过条件化信号时序逻辑（STL）规格来满足安全关键和时空约束。采用基于语法图的STL编码器、两阶段策略适配（STL条件监督微调+轨迹级偏好优化），在四旋翼导航仿真中显著提升STL满足率（24.8-40.7个百分点）同时几乎不损失自然语言任务成功率（至多1.8个百分点）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLA模型虽然能遵循自然语言指令执行任务，但自然语言指令无法精确表达安全关键或时空相关的要求（如时间约束、空间边界等）。这导致VLA在需要严格形式化约束的场景下可能产生违规行为。因此需要一种能够感知形式化需求（如STL规格）的VLA模型，在不牺牲自然语言任务性能的前提下满足额外的形式化约束。

Method: Logic-VLA包含三个核心组件：1）基于语法图的STL编码器，通过预训练捕捉时序逻辑语义；2）STL条件化的监督微调阶段，在满足约束的演示数据上进行训练；3）轨迹级偏好优化阶段，使用流匹配替代恒等偏好优化（Flow-matching surrogate for Identity Preference Optimization），在匹配的满足-违反轨迹对上优化策略。模型在推理时接受STL规格作为额外条件输入。

Result: 在闭环四旋翼导航仿真（随机化逼真环境）中，Logic-VLA相比不感知STL的基础策略，STL满足率提升了24.8到40.7个百分点，同时自然语言任务成功率最多仅下降1.8个百分点。模型还能泛化到训练中未见过的STL公式。

Conclusion: 单个VLA模型可以通过条件化形式化需求（STL规格）在不同安全/时空约束下自适应调整行为，无需为每种规格单独训练策略。Logic-VLA证明了在保持自然语言任务完成能力的同时满足形式化约束的可行性，为VLA在安全关键场景中的应用提供了新范式。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models can follow natural-language (NL) task instructions, but such instructions may not precisely specify safety-critical or spatiotemporal requirements on the resulting behavior. We introduce Logic-VLA, a formal-requirement-aware VLA that conditions on Signal Temporal Logic (STL) specifications supplied at inference time. Logic-VLA uses a syntax-graph-based STL encoder pre-trained to capture temporal logic semantics. Policy adaptation proceeds in two stages: STL-conditioned supervised fine-tuning on satisfying demonstrations is followed by trajectory-level preference optimization over matched satisfying-violating rollout pairs using a flow-matching surrogate for Identity Preference Optimization. This formulation improves formal requirement satisfaction while preserving the nominal NL task. We evaluate Logic-VLA in closed-loop quadcopter navigation simulation across randomized photorealistic environments and test generalization to STL formulas unseen during training. Across the evaluation benchmarks, Logic-VLA improves STL satisfaction rate over an STL-blind base policy by 24.8 to 40.7 percentage points (pp) while reducing nominal NL task success by at most 1.8 pp, showing that a single VLA can adapt its behavior to varying formal requirements without requiring a separate policy for each specification.

</details>


### [157] [Nonlinear Model Predictive Control for Trajectory Tracking of Differentially Flat Fixed-Wing Aerial Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20655)
*Nishanth Bobbili,Pratyaksh Rao,Luca Morando,Luca Masci,Giuseppe Loianno*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一框架，将基于微分平坦性的轨迹生成与非线性模型预测控制（NMPC）相结合，并引入风感知采样策略以增强固定翼无人机在强风条件下的轨迹跟踪鲁棒性。通过仿真与真实飞行实验验证了该框架的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定翼无人机受到非线性动力学、气动极限和环境扰动的影响，其规划与控制具有挑战性。微分平坦性虽能生成快速可行的轨迹，但以往多局限于无模型控制器，缺乏预测能力且需要大量调参。现有方法难以同时满足计算效率、预测能力和约束感知的需求。

Method: 构建一个统一框架：(1) 利用微分平坦性生成高效的参考轨迹；(2) 将轨迹生成与NMPC结合，实现具有预测能力和约束感知的控制；(3) 在NMPC框架内引入风感知采样策略，生成动态可行的参考轨迹，主动应对风扰动，同时严格满足气动和输入约束。

Result: 通过大量仿真和真实世界飞行实验验证，该框架在复杂轨迹下显著提升了跟踪精度和鲁棒性，特别是在强风条件下采用风感知采样策略时效果尤为突出。

Conclusion: 该统一框架有效弥合了基于平坦性的高效规划与预测性约束感知控制之间的鸿沟，风感知采样策略进一步增强了固定翼无人机在恶劣风环境下的适应能力，为实际部署提供了可靠方案。

Abstract: Planning and control of fixed-wing Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) are challenging due to nonlinear dynamics, aerodynamic limits, and environmental disturbances. Differential flatness offers a principled way to generate fast, feasible trajectories, but its use has largely been confined to model-free controllers, which lack predictive capabilities and demand tuning. In this paper, we propose a unified framework that integrates differential flatness-based trajectory generation with Nonlinear Model Predictive Control (NMPC), combining computationally efficient planning with predictive, constraint-aware control. To further improve robustness, we introduce a wind-aware sampling strategy embedded within the NMPC framework, enabling the generation of dynamically feasible reference trajectories that proactively account for wind disturbances while strictly enforcing aerodynamic and control input constraints. We validate the proposed framework through extensive simulations and real-world flight experiments, demonstrating improved tracking accuracy and robustness for complex trajectories, particularly when using the proposed wind-aware sampling strategy under strong wind conditions.

</details>


### [158] [Rethinking Demonstration Unlearning in Imitation Learning for Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20784)
*Jiazhuo Li,Yu Zhang,Yiming Fei,Kangkang Dong,Xiaojun Zhu,Houde Liu,Jinze Tao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"重训校准式审计"(retrain-calibrated audit)用于机器人模仿学习中的示范数据遗忘（unlearning）评估。该审计沿"行为"（edited策略与去除示范后重训策略的行为是否一致）与"证据"（审计者能否检测其训练数据）两条轴线进行，并引入一致性检验（conformal test）合并两者，提出联合重训一致性假设。实验覆盖三套真实机器人策略类与两套仿真环境、五组预注册条件，证明两轴线可在同一检查点上双向解耦，并在ACT臂上实现重定向编辑将机器人成功率恢复至18/20。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人模仿学习依赖人类示范数据，用户可能事后要求删除其中的部分样本。直接重训是自然参照但代价随策略与数据规模增长，因此需要更廉价的"编辑"算子来修改已训练策略。然而，从机器遗忘领域继承的指标（如遗忘损失或单一成员攻击）无法评估"编辑删除了什么"在闭环（closed-loop）执行层面是否真正移除，亟需一套能够可靠度量编辑效果与重训一致性的审计方法。

Method: 提出双轴线审计框架：(1) 行为轴——在匹配状态下度量编辑后策略与"去除示范重训策略"的动作散度(action divergence)，并以独立重训之间的散度作为地板(floor)进行校准；(2) 证据轴——针对每个示范执行成员攻击(membership attack)，以重训零假设为对照，同时报告排名(rank)和绝对成员丢失水平(member-loss level)，避免仅看排名导致算子在将丢失水平推高至零假设之上后被误接受。最后用共形检验(conformal test)将两轴线合并为"联合重训一致性"假设，对照足够多的独立重训舰队以在常规显著性水平下进行拒绝检验。

Result: 在五种预注册条件下、三类真实机器人策略（含ACT）与两套仿真套件上验证：(1) 两轴线可在同一检查点上双向解耦——编辑可能修复任务行为但同时证据不变，或降低证据却使行为偏离重训；(2) 在ACT臂上，"重定向编辑"(redirect edit)将盲评的机器人成功率从低水平恢复到18/20次试验。

Conclusion: 仅靠遗忘损失或单一成员攻击不足以衡量机器人示范数据编辑的撤销效果；所提出的重训校准双轴审计能同时刻画行为与证据维度，并通过共形检验给出联合一致性的统计判定。该方法为大规模机器人策略的数据删除提供了一种可扩展、统计严谨、可解释的评估范式。

Abstract: Imitation learning for robotics depends on human demonstrations, some of which people may later ask to remove. Retraining without them is the natural reference, but its cost grows with policy and dataset scale, motivating cheaper operators that edit a trained policy. Metrics inherited from machine unlearning, such as forgetting loss or a single membership attack, do not establish what an edit removed from a policy acting in closed loop. We therefore introduce a retrain-calibrated audit that reads demonstration unlearning along two axes: behavior, whether the edited policy acts like one retrained without the removed demonstrations, and evidence, whether an auditor can still detect it was trained on them. The behavior axis measures action divergence to that retrain at matched states, calibrated by a floor built from independent retrains, so a policy at the floor is as close to a retrain as retrains are to each other. The evidence axis applies a per-demonstration membership attack against a retrain null, reporting both its rank and its absolute member-loss level, since rank alone accepts operators that inflate member losses past the null. A conformal test then combines both axes into one hypothesis of joint retrain consistency, against a fleet of independent retrains large enough to reject at conventional significance. Across five preregistered conditions on three real-robot policy classes and two simulation suites, the axes dissociate in both directions on one checkpoint, as an edit may repair task behavior while leaving evidence unchanged, or reduce evidence while moving behavior away from retraining. On the ACT arm, a redirect edit restores blind-scored robot success to 18 of 20 trials.

</details>


### [159] [Natural Sit-to-Stand Motion Synthesis For Humanoids via Guided Assistance Curricula and Staged Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20823)
*Meet Pal Singh,Vyankatesh Ashtekar,Ashish Dutta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需演示或参考轨迹、基于强化学习从零合成自然人形机器人坐站转换（STS）运动的方法。通过单一PPO策略结合耦合力/椅子高度课程、逆运动学生成的大规模初始与目标位姿采样，以及源自生物力学和最优控制的奖励设计，策略在无外力评估器上跨8种椅子高度实现了超过97%的平衡站立成功率，并在深坐姿起身方面优于现有技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人从坐姿站立（STS）是一个高维、非线性的控制问题，存在无穷多种保持平衡的起身方式，这使得从零学习自然、平滑且鲁棒的STS动作极具挑战。现有方法多依赖演示或参考轨迹，缺乏从无到有的通用学习框架，因此需要一种无需外部参考即可合成自然STS运动的方法。

Method: 采用三个互补组件：(i) 耦合力/椅子高度课程——垂直骨盆辅助力在训练初期辅助轨迹探索并随训练衰减，同时解锁更高椅子，避免过早的分布偏移导致泛化崩溃；(ii) 通过从8种以上椅子高度的大规模逆运动学生成的初始与目标姿态中随机采样，增强动作鲁棒性；(iii) 设计源自生物力学与最优控制研究的奖励函数，塑造座椅离座时（seat-off）的角动量，并通过质心压力吸引函数实现支撑区域平滑过渡，确保低力矩平滑驱动。

Result: 在确定性无外力评估器上，策略在8种椅子高度下取得了超过97%的平衡站立成功率；策略在椅子高度间展现出平滑的运动泛化，并且相比现有方法能够从显著更深的坐姿成功起身。

Conclusion: 该研究证明，仅通过强化学习与精心设计的课程学习、位姿采样和生物力学启发奖励，即可从零合成自然、平滑且鲁棒的人形STS运动，无需任何演示或参考数据，为该类复杂全身运动控制问题提供了新的解决范式。

Abstract: A humanoid has infinitely many ways to stand up from sitting while maintaining balance, making sit-to-stand (STS) a challenging control problem. We synthesise natural humanoid STS motion from scratch using reinforcement learning, without demonstrations or reference trajectories. A single Proximal Policy Optimisation policy learns smooth, human-like rising driven by three complementary components. (i) A coupled force/chair-height curriculum is used. A vertical pelvis-assist force aids early trajectory exploration and decays over training. Taller chairs are unlocked with decaying assisting force. This ensures that the policy masters a viable STS trajectory at each chair height before being exposed to harder ones, avoiding the premature distribution shift that otherwise collapses generalisation. (ii) Motion robustness is achieved by randomly sampling from a large number of inverse kinematics-generated initial and target poses spanning over eight chair heights. (iii) A set of rewards is defined inspired from biomechanics and optimal control studies. They shape the robot's angular momentum for seat-off, and enable support-region transition via centre of pressure attraction function to ensure smooth low-effort actuation. On a deterministic force-free evaluator, the policy attains more than 97% balanced-standing success across eight chair heights. The policy generalises smooth motion across chair heights and enables the robot to rise from substantially deep-seated postures as compared to the state of the art.

</details>


### [160] [Demonstration-Guided Humanoid Stand-Up on an Emulated Deformable Surface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20852)
*Aniruddh Kushwah,Vyankatesh Ashtekar,Ashish Dutta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种参考引导的强化学习框架，使29自由度Unitree G1人形机器人在可变形软地面上完成站立动作，利用硬地面上录制的演示数据，通过调整MuJoCo软接触模型参数（solref/solimp）来模拟地形柔度，并联合参考轨迹跟踪与显式恢复奖励进行训练，最终在仿真中成功实现了从倒地到站立的恢复任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在软地面（如沙地、草地）上的站立恢复任务面临挑战：硬地面录制的演示无法直接转移到柔性地面上，因为地面下陷导致支撑力延迟生成，接触密集阶段（如跪地、翻身等）的动态特性与硬地面显著不同。现有方法缺乏对地形柔度的显式建模与适应策略。

Method: 提出参考引导的强化学习框架：(1) 使用MuJoCo的solref和solimp参数建模软地面接触，降低刚度并扩大名义穿透区；(2) 奖励设计包含两部分——通过残余关节位置控制进行参考运动跟踪、显式恢复目标（骨盆高度、躯干竖直度和最终姿态）；(3) 两阶段训练策略：先在硬地面上训练，再降低地形刚度进行二次训练，使策略适应软地面的延迟支撑力生成。

Result: 在仿真中成功完成从倒地到站立任务，达到目标骨盆高度和躯干竖直度，最大接触穿透约40mm。该方法在两个站立序列上均成功实现硬地面和软地面上的最终恢复目标。消融实验表明仅有参考跟踪不足以保证成功站立，显式恢复奖励是必要的。

Conclusion: 参考引导的强化学习结合显式恢复奖励与软接触建模，能够有效将硬地面演示迁移到软地面站立任务中，为可变形地面下人形机器人运动生成提供了可行方案。

Abstract: This paper presents a reference-guided reinforcement learning framework to generate stand-up motion for a 29-DOF Unitree G1 humanoid on deformable soft ground, using a human demonstration recorded on hard ground. The terrain compliance is modelled using solref and solimp parameters from MuJoCo's rigid body soft-contact model. The rewards consists of (i) reference motion tracking through residual joint-position control and (ii) explicit recovery objectives such as pelvis height, torso uprightness, and the final posture. First, the policy is trained with the specified rewards considering hard ground. Next, the terrain stiffness is lowered by updating solref and the nominal surface penetration zone is expanded using solimp. Subsequent training enables the policy to adapt to the delayed support force generation due to significant surface penetration during contact-intensive phases while preserving the original demonstration pattern. The learned policy successfully completes the fallen-to-standing task in simulation, reaching the targeted pelvis height and uprightness, with a maximum contact penetration of approximately 40 mm during the process. The proposed method is demonstrated on two stand-up sequences and successfully achieves the final recovery objective on both hard and soft ground. Ablation studies show that reference tracking alone is insufficient for successful stand-up, and that explicit recovery rewards are essential.

</details>


### [161] [IMU-Free Body-Frame State Estimation with Sparse Scene Flow for Quadcopters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20891)
*Daniel Grønhaug,Sofie Markeset,Mathias Kolberg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套纯视觉状态估计系统，适用于配备标准双目相机、无惯性传感器的X构型四旋翼无人机。系统完全在机体坐标系下运行，仅需同步的双目图像和电机推力指令。连续-离散扩展卡尔曼滤波器在复合流形状态上维持机体位姿、速度、角速度、重力和扰动的估计，以静止场景点作为隐式惯性参考。通过χ²门控筛选静止特征点，并通过4视图束调整生成带位置、速度和联合协方差的稀疏3D点云，实现空间自适应密度的场景流输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统无人机状态估计依赖GPS和IMU等惯性传感器，但这类传感器在GNSS受限环境（如室内、地下、城市峡谷）中不可用或不可靠。同时，纯视觉状态估计面临尺度不确定性、重力方向不可观测等问题。本文旨在探索一种无惯性传感器的视觉状态估计方案，利用机载立体视觉和推力命令在机体坐标系内完成自运动估计与场景流生成，为下游世界模型提供测量源。

Method: 系统由三部分组成：(1) 连续-离散扩展卡尔曼滤波器，定义在复合流形状态⟨SE(3), R^3, …⟩上，融合双目特征点观测与推力命令，估计机体位姿、速度、角速度、重力及扰动；以静止场景点作为隐式惯性参考。(2) 特征跟踪与匹配模块：FAST/Shi-Tomasi检测、SSD/Lucas-Kanade时间跟踪、NCC跨相机匹配，利用滤波器预测的搜索区域缩减匹配范围，并通过归一化新息的χ²门控仅接受静止点。(3) 4视图（两个时间戳的双目对）全束调整，联合估计特征点的位置和速度，以滤波器的相对位姿为先验，生成携带位置、速度和联合协方差的稀疏点云，密度由外部焦点引导实现空间自适应分配。

Result: 系统在无GPS、IMU和世界坐标系基础设施的条件下，成功实现了机体坐标系内的状态估计与稀疏场景流生成。输出包括机体坐标系状态估计、校准后的位姿变化以及稀疏场景流。点云通过4视图束调整在空间上自适应分布，聚焦区域密集覆盖，其他区域稀疏覆盖。特征点信息通过位姿先验间接融入求解器，实现了EKF与束调整的高效解耦。

Conclusion: 该纯视觉状态估计系统证明了在无惯性传感器前提下，利用双目立体视觉、推力命令和静止场景点作为隐式参考，足以在机体坐标系内实现一致的状态估计与场景流生成。架构设计留有未来集成GPS/IMU的扩展空间，可作为基于当前机体坐标系的下游世界模型的测量源。

Abstract: We present a vision-only state estimation system for X-configuration quadcopters equipped with a canonical stereo camera pair and no inertial sensors. The system operates entirely in the body frame, requiring only synchronised stereo images and motor thrust commands. A continuous-discrete extended Kalman filter on a composite manifold state $\langle SE(3), \mathbb{R}^3, \ldots \rangle$ maintains estimates of body-frame pose, velocity, angular velocity, gravity, and disturbances, using stationary scene points as implicit inertial references. Feature points are detected (FAST, Shi-Tomasi), tracked temporally (SSD, Lucas-Kanade) and matched across cameras (NCC), with search regions predicted from filter-derived pose and point uncertainty. Chi-squared gating on the normalised innovation admits only stationary points to the filter. The system also produces a sparse 3D point cloud carrying per-point position, velocity and joint covariance. These come from a 4-view (two stereo pairs at two timestamps) full bundle adjustment that jointly estimates position and velocity from stereo disparity and temporal parallax, with the filter-derived relative pose as a prior. Feature points in the EKF do not enter the solver; their information is reflected through the pose prior. Point cloud density is spatially adaptive: an external focus point directs allocation, producing dense coverage in the region of attention and sparse coverage elsewhere. The output is a body-frame state estimate, a calibrated pose change, and a sparse scene flow. It is intended as a measurement source for a downstream world model anchored in the current body frame, without dependence on GPS, IMU, or any world-frame infrastructure, though the architecture accommodates their future integration.

</details>


### [162] [Scalable Distributed Simulation-Based Testing for Automated Driving Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20904)
*Christian Geller,Benedikt Haas,Lutz Eckstein*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个端到端的、DevOps驱动的框架，用于在轻量级Kubernetes集群上自动化构建、部署和分布式执行CARLA场景测试。通过Helm图表打包ROS 2应用、Helmfile声明式组合仿真环境、Argo Workflows编排分布式测试流程。在200个场景的多节点K3s集群评估中，最佳配置比顺序基线提速超过8倍，并量化了并行度、编排开销与集群稳定性之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虚拟场景测试是验证自动驾驶系统（ADS）和智能交通系统（ITS）的关键手段，然而大规模测试套件涉及数千个场景，执行过程劳动密集且难以扩展，需要自动化的分布式解决方案来提升测试效率。

Method: 该框架包括三个核心组件：(1) 将ROS 2应用打包为标准化的Kubernetes Helm图表（从仓库规范自动生成）；(2) 通过动态Helmfile清单声明式组合完整仿真环境；(3) 利用Argo Workflows实现分布式测试工作流，包括环境供给、OpenSCENARIO测试用例的聚合与批处理、跨集群节点的并行场景执行，以及日志和资源指标的收集。

Result: 在多节点K3s集群上运行200个场景的评估中，最佳配置将端到端工作流时间较顺序基线提升超过8倍。同时量化了并行度、编排开销与集群稳定性之间的权衡关系，并在真实ADS测试应用中验证了框架与场景源及下游评估模块的对接能力。

Conclusion: 该框架不仅为可扩展的仿真测试提供了坚实基础，还能生成可追溯的证据以支持安全论证，展示了其在ADS验证中的实用价值。

Abstract: Virtual scenario-based testing is a key enabler for validating automated driving systems (ADS) and intelligent transport systems (ITS). However, executing large-scale test suites involving possibly thousands of scenarios remains labor-intensive and difficult to scale. This paper presents an end-to-end, DevOps-driven framework that automates build, deployment, and distributed execution of CARLA-based scenario tests of an ADS on a lightweight Kubernetes cluster. ROS 2 applications are packaged as standardized Kubernetes Helm charts generated from repository specifications, while entire simulation environments are composed declaratively via dynamic Helmfile manifests. The paper describes how a distributed testing workflow can be implemented in Argo Workflows to provision environments, aggregate and batch OpenSCENARIO test cases from configurable sources, execute scenarios in parallel across cluster nodes, and collect logs and resource metrics. In an evaluation on a multi-node K3s cluster running 200 scenarios, the best configuration speeds up end-to-end workflow time by more than a factor of eight compared to a sequential baseline. The results demonstrate significant gains in end-to-end execution time and quantify trade-offs between parallelism, orchestration overhead, and cluster stability. The framework is further demonstrated in a real-world ADS test application with connections to scenario sources and downstream evaluation modules. This demonstrates that the approach provides a strong foundation not only for scalable simulation testing, but also for generating traceable evidence that can support safety arguments.

</details>


### [163] [Fast Coordinated Bimanual Motion Planning With Hard Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20946)
*Borna Paro,Luka Petrović,Ivan Marković*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种快速双臂运动规划管线，采用领导者-跟随者参数化方法，通过逆运动学连续满足刚性物体双臂操作中的硬性变换约束，在仿真中比先前工作快19.4倍，并在真实Kinova Gen3双臂平台上验证了直接轨迹迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双臂操作任务涉及高自由度，且当操作刚性物体时，两个末端执行器之间的相对变换必须在整个运动过程中保持不变，这构成了非线性等式约束，将可行配置空间限制在零测度流形上，给传统运动规划器带来极大挑战。现有方法难以高效地满足这一硬约束。

Method: 提出领导者-跟随者参数化方案：将领导者的构型视为自由变量，而跟随者的构型通过逆向运动学确定以满足刚性约束。该硬变换约束在整条路径上被连续强制满足，而非仅在离散采样点处满足。

Result: 在多种仿真环境、约束条件和双臂平台上进行了广泛评估，规划速度比先前工作快19.4倍，同时保证约束的连续满足。在Kinova Gen3双臂平台上的真实实验中，包括托盘运输和细长物体操作，验证了规划轨迹可直接迁移至物理硬件。

Conclusion: 所提出的双臂运动规划管线通过领导者-跟随者参数化和基于逆运动学的约束满足机制，在保证硬性相对变换约束全程连续成立的同时，显著提升了规划效率，且规划轨迹可直接部署于真实双臂机器人，具备良好的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Bimanual manipulation enables complex tasks but introduces added complexity from the high number of degrees of freedom involved. When handling rigid objects, the relative transformation between the two end effectors must remain fixed throughout the motion, manifesting as a nonlinear equality constraint that confines the feasible configuration space to a measure-zero manifold and challenges conventional motion planners. We propose a fast bimanual motion planning pipeline that enforces this hard transformation constraint continuously along the entire path, using a leader-follower parameterization: the leader's configuration is treated as a free variable, while the follower's is determined via inverse kinematics to satisfy the constraint. We extensively evaluate the method in simulation across diverse environments, constraints and bimanual platforms, achieving 19.4x faster planning than prior work while guaranteeing continuous constraint satisfaction. Real-world experiments on a bimanual Kinova Gen3 setup, involving tray transport and elongated-object manipulation, validate direct transfer of planned trajectories to physical hardware.

</details>


### [164] [Neural-Primitive: An Efficient End-to-end Local Planner with Primitive-based Imitation Learning for Autonomous Flight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20948)
*Zhitao Liu,Guangtong Xu,Zihan Wang,Jialiang Hou,Chao Xu,Fei Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于模仿学习的端到端局部规划器，通过轻量级离线基元数据集收集框架生成安全高质量轨迹基元，紧凑神经网络直接映射感知输入到多项式系数，实现无线后端求解的实时轨迹生成，计算低于1ms且内存小于1.5MiB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主飞行在未知杂乱环境中面临计算质量与内存的三难困境——传统在线轨迹生成方法需要在计算效率、轨迹质量和内存占用之间进行折中，难以同时满足机上实时规划的所有需求。

Method: 1) 设计轻量级离线基元（offline-primitive）数据集收集框架，在非凸环境中生成安全高质量的轨迹基元；2) 训练紧凑神经网络将传感器输入直接映射为多项式系数，隐式编码高阶动力学信息；3) 学习到的策略无需后端求解即可生成平滑、经验性无碰撞且动力学可行的轨迹。

Result: 在标准桌面端计算延迟低于1ms，机载飞行中平均3.68ms；机上内存占用小于1.5MiB；大量仿真基准测试表明规划延迟和目标到达进度质量方面均具优越性；真实世界零样本部署验证了鲁棒的仿真到现实迁移能力。

Conclusion: 该端到端模仿学习局部规划器成功解决了计算-质量-内存三难困境，通过离线基元数据收集加轻量网络推理实现超快、低内存且高质量的实时轨迹生成，并具备良好的Sim-to-Real迁移能力。

Abstract: Autonomous flight in unknown cluttered environments is hindered by the computation-quality-memory trilemma of onboard trajectory generation. In this paper, we propose an efficient end-to-end local planner via imitation learning. A lightweight offline-primitive-based dataset collection framework is designed to produce safe and high-quality trajectory primitives in non-convex environments. A compact neural network directly maps sensory inputs to polynomial coefficients that inherently encode higher-order dynamical information. The learned policy generates smooth, empirically collision-free and dynamically feasible trajectories in real time without back-end solving. It achieves ultra-fast computation (below 1ms on a standard desktop and average 3.68ms during onboard flight), while maintaining low onboard memory requirements (less than 1.5MiB). Extensive simulation benchmarks demonstrate superiority in both planning latency and target-reaching progress quality. Zero-shot deployment in real-world experiments further validates the robust sim-to-real transfer capability of the proposed method.

</details>


### [165] [Hybrid Roller-Jamming Gripper for Object Acquisition and Retention Under Pose Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20962)
*Yijie Ren,Guillaume Gourmelen,Hiroyasu Iwata*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合滚轮-颗粒堵塞（hybrid roller-jamming）夹爪，将主动物体吸入与抓取后固定能力集成于单一夹爪中，在840次抓取试验中成功812次，验证了该策略在非理想首次接触下提升物体抓取与保持性能的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 家庭环境中的物体操作常因位姿不确定性导致首次接触偏离中心或不完全接触，使可靠抓取变得困难。现有方案各有局限：滚轮式夹爪能主动吸入物体但抓取后稳定性不足，颗粒堵塞夹爪则需要在堵塞前建立充分接触才能实现强保持力。

Method: 提出混合滚轮-颗粒堵塞夹爪，通过内向滚轮旋转增加接触面积并将物体拉向夹爪中心，随后利用真空诱导的颗粒堵塞使滚轮变硬并稳定抓取。研究包括简化几何分析、样机力能力基准测试，以及在7自由度机械臂上进行8种测试物体的评估，施加受控的平面位置与姿态偏移（每种条件重复3次）。

Result: 主评估包含840次抓取试验（216次平面偏移、624次姿态偏移），总体成功率812/840（平面偏移215/216，姿态偏移597/624）。消融评估共162次试验，滚轮单独模式与堵塞单独模式分别成功54/81和24/81次，显示了两者不同的贡献。

Conclusion: 实验结果为混合滚轮-堵塞策略提供了初步的机构层面证据，表明其是改善非完美首次接触后物体获取与保持性能的有效方案，值得进一步研究。

Abstract: In household manipulation, pose uncertainty often results in off-centre or partial initial contact, making reliable object acquisition difficult. Roller-based grippers can actively draw objects inward but often provide limited post-capture stability, whereas granular-jamming grippers require sufficient contact before jamming to achieve strong retention. This paper presents a hybrid roller-jamming gripper that integrates active object intake and post-capture retention within a single gripper. The proposed gripper uses inward roller rotation to increase contact and draw the object toward the gripper centre, followed by vacuum-induced granular jamming to stiffen the rollers and stabilise the grasp. The paper also presents a simplified geometric analysis of the gripper and a bench-level characterisation of the prototype's force capability. The gripper prototype was mounted on a 7-DoF robotic arm and evaluated using eight test objects. Furthermore, controlled planar position and orientation offsets were applied, with each condition repeated three times. The main evaluation comprised 840 grasp trials, including 216 planar-offset trials and 624 orientation-offset trials. Overall, the gripper succeeded in 812/840 trials: 215/216 planar-offset trials and 597/624 orientation-offset trials. The ablation evaluation comprised 162 trials on three objects. The roller-only and jamming-only conditions achieved 54/81 and 24/81 successes, respectively, showing their different contributions. These results provide initial mechanism-level evidence that hybrid roller-jamming is a promising strategy for improving acquisition and retention after imperfect first contact.

</details>


### [166] [PhysCaP: Grounding Code-as-Policy Agent with Physics-Informed Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21031)
*Chen-Yu Lin,Jing-Wen Chen,Hsueh-En Chang,Hung-An Chen,Sheng-Hsun Chang,Chi-Pin Huang,Fu-En Yang,Min-Hung Chen,Yi-Ting Chen,Yu-Chiang Frank Wang,Shao-Hua Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PhysCaP，一个物理信息驱动的代码即策略（Code-as-Policy）智能体，用于机器人操作中的主动感知。通过物理信息探索层实现显式信息寻求，利用训练-free的物理属性提取模块从机器人本体感受估计质量和刚度，并采用双智能体设计（Planner决定探索时机，Prioritizer过滤和排序交互）平衡探索成本与信息效率。在真实桌面操作任务和LIBERO模拟任务中，PhysCaP以更少的交互和更短执行时间达到与基线相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略虽然擅长模仿演示，但依赖被动观察，无法推断操作中至关重要的潜在物理属性（如物体质量、刚度等），导致在面对物理属性隐藏的场景时表现不佳。

Method: PhysCaP在代码即策略框架上增加了物理信息探索层。核心包括：(1) 训练-free的物理属性提取模块，仅利用机器人本体感受（proprioception）估计物体质量和刚度，无需额外传感器；(2) 双智能体设计：Planner决定何时探索、何时停止；Prioritizer过滤不合理的交互并基于启发式优先级分数对剩余交互排序，实现高效、有目标的探索。

Result: 在真实桌面操作任务（搜索隐藏物体、检测空罐、寻找成熟牛油果）和LIBERO模拟任务上评估。结果表明：现有的被动基线和朴素交互基线在物理属性隐藏时要么失败，要么过度探索；PhysCaP以更少的交互次数和更短执行时间达到相当性能。消融实验验证了物理属性提取模块的有效性。

Conclusion: PhysCaP通过主动物理信息探索有效解决了被动VLA策略无法推断潜在物理属性的问题，在保证性能的同时显著减少交互次数和执行时间，为机器人操作中的主动感知提供了新思路。

Abstract: We present PhysCaP, a Physics-Informed Code-as-Policy agent for active perception in robotic manipulation. While vision-language-action policies excel at imitating demonstrations, they rely on passive observation and fail to infer latent physical properties critical for manipulation. PhysCaP augments code-as-policy frameworks with a physics-informed exploration layer that enables explicit information-seeking through interaction. It introduces training-free physical property extraction modules that estimate object mass and stiffness from robot proprioception without additional sensors. To balance exploration costs and the efficiency of information obtained, PhysCaP employs a dual-agent design: a Planner that decides when to explore and when to stop, and a Prioritizer that filters implausible interactions and ranks the remainder using a heuristic priority score, enabling efficient, targeted exploration. We evaluate PhysCaP on real-world tabletop manipulation tasks (searching for hidden objects, detecting empty cans, and finding ripe avocados) and a simulated task in LIBERO. The results show that existing passive and naive interactive baselines either fail when physical properties are hidden or over-explore, whereas PhysCaP achieves comparable performance with fewer interactions and reduced execution time. Ablation studies further validate the effectiveness of the proposed physical property extraction modules. Project page: https://physcap.github.io

</details>


### [167] [Roadside-Cooperative Autonomous Driving: From Data Platform to Vision-Language End-to-End Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21032)
*Yitao Xu,Tong Wu,Yiyan Wu,Guoji Xu,Yanbo Jiang,Jiahao Wang,Zehong Ke,Junkai Jiang,Fang Zhang,Jianqiang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了V2XBench仿真平台与Chat-V2XBench数据集，并在此基础上构建了AURORA端到端协同驾驶框架，通过Cross-View查询对齐融合模块与LoRA适配的视觉语言模型，在遮挡场景下实现SOTA性能（路线完成率98.21%，驾驶得分76.02），开创了V2X-VLM协同自动驾驶新范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有V2X基准缺乏闭环评估与语言监督支持，阻碍了视觉语言模型在端到端协同驾驶中的发展。需要提出一个支持闭环评估和语言基础的仿真平台及数据集，以推动该领域研究。

Method: 作者提出V2XBench仿真平台（含同步自车-路侧感知与闭环评估）和Chat-V2XBench逐步结构化的VQA数据集。在此基础上提出AURORA框架，采用双视角感知架构，通过Cross-View Query Alignment and Fusion（CQAF）模块在查询级别对齐融合自车与路侧视角的时空及语义差异，利用统一token通过LoRA适配的VLM桥接语义推理与生成式轨迹规划。

Result: 在V2XBench上的闭环评估显示，AURORA在重度遮挡场景下实现SOTA性能，路线完成率达98.21%，驾驶得分76.02，且所需路侧通信带宽较低。

Conclusion: 该工作开创了可扩展的V2X-VLM协同范式，为下一代协同自动驾驶铺平道路。

Abstract: Vehicle-to-Everything (V2X) cooperation enables beyond-line-of-sight perception, mitigating occlusions in single-vehicle sensing. However, existing V2X benchmarks provide limited support for closed-loop evaluation and language-grounded supervision, hindering the development of vision-language models (VLMs) for end-to-end cooperative driving. To address these limitations, we introduce V2XBench, a simulation platform featuring synchronized ego--roadside sensing and closed-loop evaluation, together with Chat-V2XBench, a progressively structured VQA dataset for cooperative reasoning. Building upon this benchmark infrastructure, we propose AURORA, an end-to-end cooperative driving framework. Equipped with a dual-view perception architecture, AURORA mitigates spatial and semantic discrepancies across ego and roadside viewpoints through a query-level Cross-View Query Alignment and Fusion (CQAF) module. Leveraging the resulting unified tokens, a LoRA-adapted VLM bridges semantic reasoning and generative trajectory planning. Extensive closed-loop evaluations on V2XBench demonstrate that AURORA achieves state-of-the-art performance in heavily occluded scenarios, with a Route Completion rate of 98.21% and a Driving Score of 76.02, while requiring low roadside communication bandwidth. Ultimately, this work pioneers an extensible V2X--VLM paradigm, paving the way for next-generation cooperative autonomous driving.

</details>


### [168] [TaPeR: Probabilistic Recovery of Sparse Task Precedence Graphs from a Handful of Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21035)
*Adrian Röfer,Karla Stepanova,Abhinav Valada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种仅利用运动学图和物体相对姿态分布来从人类演示中提取任务依赖结构的方法，无需符号谓词标注，并通过过滤管线将概率图转换为最终的任务依赖图，从而支持机器人灵活重排子任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期操作任务往往只有部分有序，恢复子任务间的依赖关系可让机器人灵活重排子任务并保持任务有效性。然而现有方法需要时间和符号监督，符号谓词显式接地在现实场景中难以获取。

Method: 该方法仅使用简单运动学图和物体相对姿态分布来提取任务依赖结构。首先从这些表征中估计成对任务步骤依赖概率，并以此初始化前驱图（precedence graph）的边权重；然后引入一个过滤管线，将该概率估计图转换为最终的任务依赖图。

Result: 在现有基准和包含更长任务及更复杂依赖关系的新数据集上进行了评估，结果表明该方法能用更少的演示恢复出比基线更准确的任务结构；此外，推断出的图可用于为同一任务生成多个有效的机器人执行顺序。

Conclusion: 该方法验证了仅依赖廉价的运动学与姿态信息即可可靠恢复任务依赖结构，为部分有序的长期操作任务提供了无需符号监督的可扩展解决方案，并支持灵活生成多个有效的执行顺序。

Abstract: Long-horizon manipulation tasks are often only partially ordered. For example, when assembling an electronic device, the battery and circuit board may be installed in either order, but both must be in place before the enclosure is closed. Recovering such dependencies enables robots to flexibly reorder subtasks while preserving task validity. Existing approaches typically infer task structure from human demonstrations using both temporal and symbolic supervision. However, symbolic predicates require explicit grounding, which is difficult to obtain in realistic settings. In this work, we present an approach for extracting task dependency structures from demonstrations using only simple kinematic graphs and distributions over relative object poses. From these representations, our method estimates pairwise task-step-dependency probabilities and uses them to initialize the edge weights of a precedence graph. We then introduce a filtering pipeline that converts this graph of probability estimates into the final task dependency graph. We evaluate our approach on an existing benchmark and on a new dataset comprising longer tasks with more complex dependencies. We find that our method recovers more accurate task structures from fewer demonstrations than the baselines. Finally, we demonstrate that the inferred graphs can be used to generate multiple valid robotic execution orders for the same task.

</details>


### [169] [FF-MPCC: High-speed Agile Formation Flight with Model Predictive Contouring Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21056)
*Aditya Dandwate,Vit Kratky,Parakh M. Gupta,Martin Saska,Robert Penicka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于MPCC框架的去中心化编队飞行方法，通过重新参数化和同步动态编队几何，使无人机在遵循高要求轨迹时保持编队完整性，在高达21 m/s的高速飞行中编队保持性能提升65%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机在执行高动态轨迹（接近平台极限飞行）时，如何敏捷地保持规定编队仍是一个挑战。现有的时间参数化轨迹跟踪方法无法在高速条件下兼顾编队保持与个体动态约束。

Method: 1) 将编队保持集成到MPCC（模型预测轮廓控制）框架中；2) 提出一种新的重新参数化和同步方法处理动态编队几何；3) 提出去中心化方法确定各无人机期望位置，使编队沿复杂路径协调高速跟踪。

Result: 通过广泛仿真和真实世界实验验证，涵盖不同复杂度路径和飞行中编队形状变化。与时间参数化轨迹跟踪相比，在高达21 m/s的高速飞行中编队保持改善65%，同时到达目标的时间相当。

Conclusion: 所提出的去中心化MPCC编队方法能够在高速条件下保持编队完整性，同时适应复杂路径和动态编队形状变化，显著优于传统时间参数化方法。

Abstract: Flying in a prescribed formation in an agile manner remains a challenging problem in the field of UAVs, particularly when following highly-demanding trajectories that require flight at platform limits. We address this problem by proposing a novel decentralized approach to formation flight along a given path that integrates formation maintenance into the MPCC framework, allowing UAVs to adapt their progression along complex paths while respecting individual dynamic constraints and maintaining the desired formation. To this end, we introduce a novel reparametrization and synchronization method for dynamic formation geometries together with a decentralized approach to determine the desired positions for the individual UAVs. The proposed approach allows the formation to coordinate high-speed path following without compromising formation integrity. The proposed approach is validated through extensive simulation and real-world experiments involving scenarios with varying complexity of paths and changes of required formation shape on the fly. In comparison to time-parameterized trajectory tracking, we demonstrate improved formation maintenance by 65% in high-speed flight with velocities up to 21 m/s, while achieving comparable times required to reach the goal.

</details>


### [170] [Teaching is a Process: The TOSS Framework for Modeling Human Teaching Decisions in Human-Interactive Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21083)
*Bernhard Hilpert,Kim Baraka,Joost Broekens*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过一项N=34的探索性自下而上研究，分析204条人类在机器人强化学习场景中的直觉教学响应，揭示教学决策由触发因素、目标、信号和策略构成的相互关联网络，并提出TOSS框架将人机教学建模为可循环的触发-信号响应过程，为理解教学决策、模拟教学预言机以及设计以人为本的机器人学习算法提供理论基础和公开数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成功的人机教学需要机器人的处理需求与人类教学意图之间保持一致，但当前对这一内在对齐机制的理解尚不充分。研究旨在揭示人类在直觉教学时背后所采用的底层逻辑，以便更好地理解和设计人机教学系统。

Method: 采用探索性、自下而上的方法，招募34名参与者观察两个不同的机器人强化学习场景，收集204条涵盖早、中、晚学习阶段的直觉教学响应，并对这些响应进行质性分析以识别教学决策中的结构模式。

Result: 教学决策由触发因素、目标、信号和策略构成的相互关联的深层网络组成；人类教师会自发扮演教练、工程师或设计师等不同角色，并优先考虑不同的教学目标；据此提出TOSS框架，将人机教学概念化为机器人行为与人类教学行为之间的程序化循环。

Conclusion: TOSS框架为理解教学决策提供了理论框架，并为未来研究提供了三个方向：模拟真实教学教学预言机、设计以人为中心的教学环境，以及开发超越当前机器人学习设置局限的新型机器人学习算法；同时公开了数据集以供社区使用。

Abstract: Successful Human-Robot Teaching assumes alignment between robot processing needs and human teaching intent. To better understand this alignment, this work seeks to uncover the underlying logic that humans intuitively apply when teaching. Through an exploratory, bottom-up study with N=34, participants observing two distinct robot Reinforcement Learning (RL) scenarios, we analyze 204 intuitive teaching responses across early, middle, and late learning phases. Results reveal that teaching decisions consist of a nuanced, interconnected network of Triggers (situational catalysts), Objectives (subjective teaching targets), Signals (communicative acts), and Strategies (high-level governance) in which teachers spontaneously adopt diverse roles, acting as coaches, engineers, or designers and prioritize different objectives. Based on these results, we introduce the TOSS Framework, which conceptualizes Human-Robot teaching as a procedural loop between robot behavior and human teaching actions, in which human teaching decisions are modeled as Trigger-Signal responses modulated by teaching Objectives and Strategies. It provides future research with an openly accessible dataset and a theoretical foundation for a) understanding teaching decisions and b) simulating realistic oracles as well as c) designing human-centered teaching settings and novel robot learning algorithms that go beyond the constraints of current robot learning settings.

</details>


### [171] [SRL-MPC: Shape-Aware Reinforcement Learned Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21175)
*Ruihua Han,Rui Gao,Zhe Liu,Xinyi Wang,Chang Chen,Shuai Wang,Qi Hao,Jia Pan,Hengshuang Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SRL-MPC方法，结合高阶级控制屏障函数与强化学习，实现异构形状机器人在密集人群中的安全高效导航，无需几何简化，显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统形状感知导航方法通常假设机器人同质、工作空间稀疏、几何简化或依赖离线计算与手工参数，难以应对密集人群及异构机器人编队场景，限制了实际部署。

Method: 1) 基于支撑函数变换提取几何分离特征（GSFs）构建高阶级控制屏障函数（HOCBF）约束以编码形状感知安全性；2) 利用强化学习（RL）学习读取GSFs的神经策略，实时输出MPC参数更新，使MPC求解器自适应邻居人群几何形状。

Result: 在随机人群场景及任意形状机器人编队实验中，SRL-MPC在安全性、可扩展性和鲁棒性方面均优于代表性基线方法，尤其在安全性和适应性上表现突出。

Conclusion: SRL-MPC兼顾了MPC的安全结构保障与泛化能力以及RL的适应性与智能性，为异构人群中的形状感知导航提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Safe and efficient shape-aware navigation in heterogeneous crowds and robot fleets remains challenging. Traditional approaches often assume homogeneous robots, sparse workspaces, simplified geometry, offline computation, or handcrafted parameters to make the problem tractable, which limits their deployment in dense crowd scenarios. Toward this end, we propose Shape-Aware Reinforcement Learned Model Predictive Control (SRL-MPC), a method for safe, efficient, and adaptive navigation in crowds with heterogeneous shapes without geometry simplification. To encode shape-aware safety, we formulate high-order control barrier function (HOCBF) constraints from geometric separation features (GSFs) based on support function transformation. A reinforcement learning (RL) framework then learns a neural policy that reads GSFs and outputs real-time MPC parameter updates, enabling the MPC solver to adapt to neighboring crowd geometries. The key advantage of SRL-MPC is that it preserves the safety structure and generalizability of MPC while integrating the adaptability and intelligence of RL. Experiments in randomized crowd scenarios with arbitrary shaped robot fleets demonstrate the effectiveness, scalability, and robustness of SRL-MPC. The results show that SRL-MPC substantially outperforms representative baselines in safety and adaptability. Project website: https://hanruihua.github.io/srl_mpc_project/

</details>


### [172] [Beyond Imitation: Self-Improving Robot Policies via Off-Policy Q-Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21204)
*Varun Giridhar,Anant Khandelwal,Jeremy A. Collins,Ignat Georgiev,Animesh Garg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Q-Planning通过为大型视觉运动行为克隆策略配备一个小型离策略Q函数，实现无需人类干预的在线自我改进。Q函数训练于成功演示并吸收成功与失败的部署轨迹，在推理时进行Q值引导的动作选择，在线迭代中仅微调Q函数而冻结BC权重。在LIBERO、RoboTwin及双臂真实机器人接触丰富任务上均显著提升表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为克隆（BC）在机器人操作中取得显著进展，但本质局限在于无法自我改进——策略失败时无法从失败中学习，需要额外的人类演示。强化学习微调提供了自我改进的路径，但难以扩展到现代机器人策略所依赖的数十亿参数模型。

Method: Q-Planning将大型视觉运动BC策略与一个小型离策略Q函数配对。Q函数估计价值而非模仿动作，因此既可在与BC策略相同的成功演示上训练，又能吸收成功和失败的部署轨迹（BC不具备这种不对称性）。利用这一不对称性实现两个功能：（1）推理时的价值引导动作选择——对BC采样结果进行单步Q值加权平均；（2）在线自我改进——仅微调Q函数，BC权重保持冻结。

Result: 在LIBERO和双臂RoboTwin上，十次自改进迭代提升了所有测试基准分数（LIBERO-10从93%提升至99%，RoboTwin从83.8%提升至91.4%），并缩短了接近上限套件（LIBERO-Object、LIBERO-Goal）的成功回合时长。在两个接触丰富的双臂真实机器人任务中，相同的循环（BC冻结、无人干预）仅凭自身部署轨迹实现改进：stack-cups从40%提升至90%，insert-wallet从25%提升至80%（五次迭代），而仅对成功轨迹进行SFT则停滞在55%和30%。在相同在线预算下，Q-Planning是Best-of-N、filtered SFT、IBRL、DSRL和DAWR中唯一无需训练辅助actor即能从失败中稳定改进的方法。

Conclusion: Q-Planning通过利用Q函数在"价值估计"而非"动作模仿"上的不对称性，成功实现了对大规模BC策略的稳定在线自改进，且无需人类干预、无需微调BC权重或训练辅助actor，在仿真与真实机器人任务中均验证了有效性。

Abstract: Behaviour Cloning (BC) has driven remarkable progress in robot manipulation, yet it is fundamentally limited by its inability to self-improve: a policy that fails cannot learn from that failure without additional human demonstrations. Reinforcement Learning fine-tuning offers a path to self-improvement but has proven difficult to scale to the multi-billion-parameter models underpinning modern robot policies. We propose Q-Planning, which equips a large visuomotor BC policy with a small off-policy Q-function. Because a Q-function estimates value rather than imitates actions, it can be trained on the same successful demonstrations as the BC policy and later absorb both successful and failed deployment rollouts, an asymmetry BC does not have. We exploit this asymmetry to enable value-guided action selection at inference (a single-step Q-weighted average over BC draws) and online self-improvement that fine-tunes only the Q-function, leaving the BC weights untouched. On LIBERO and bimanual RoboTwin, ten iterations of self-improvement lift every benchmark score we tested (LIBERO-10 93% to 99%, RoboTwin 83.8% to 91.4%) and shorten successful episodes on the near-ceiling suites (LIBERO-Object, LIBERO-Goal). On two contact-rich bimanual real-robot tasks, the same loop (BC frozen, no human intervention) improves purely from its own deployment rollouts: stack-cups 40% to 90% and insert-wallet 25% to 80% in five iterations, whereas SFT on successful rollouts alone stalls at 55% and 30%. Under an identical online budget Q-Planning is the only method, among Best-of-N, filtered SFT, IBRL, DSRL, and DAWR, that improves stably from failures without training an auxiliary actor.

</details>


### [173] [The Coastline as a Structural Constraint: Harnessing Scene Geometry for Autonomous Surface Vessel Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21276)
*Derek R. Benham,Joshua G. Mangelson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出两种利用海岸线和水面几何结构实现的GPS拒止环境下的无人船自定位框架：一种基于LiDAR观测水面估计横滚、俯仰和垂荡，并通过海岸线注册到卫星地图恢复全局位姿；另一种仅依赖被动图像语义分割检测海岸线和地平线，利用单目图像推断海岸线距离，构建局部子图并注册到卫星海岸线地图，在分层因子图中融合。实验表明LiDAR管线提升了轨迹精度，单目架构保持了有界长期漂移，且零样本基础模型可可靠提取海岸线观测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPS拒止环境下无人船定位困难，而沿海环境蕴含丰富的几何结构（海岸线、水面几何）尚未被充分利用，可提供全局参考定位线索。

Method: 提出两套互补框架：(1) LiDAR框架：利用水面LiDAR观测估计横滚、俯仰、垂荡，通过海岸线观测直接配准到卫星海岸线地图恢复全局位置和航向；(2) 单目视觉框架：利用语义分割检测海岸线和地平线，基于沿海场景几何从单目图像推断海岸线距离，累积短时局部子图，配准到卫星海岸线地图，并在分层因子图中融合。

Result: 在三个真实沿海数据集上评估：LiDAR管线相比标准基线一致提升了轨迹精度；单目架构维持有界长期漂移；零样本基础模型能可靠提取多类沿海环境的海岸线观测。

Conclusion: 沿海几何是GPS拒止海事定位中强大且可靠的全局参考信息来源。

Abstract: Coastal environments contain rich, largely unexploited geometric structure capable of providing globally referenced localization cues. In this work, we present two complementary localization frameworks that exploit shoreline and water-surface geometry for GPS-denied autonomous surface vessel localization. The first framework leverages LiDAR observations of the water surface to estimate roll, pitch, and heave (vertical motion), while recovering global position and heading through direct registration of shoreline observations against a satellite-derived coastline map. The second framework relies solely on passive imagery to detect the shoreline and horizon through semantic segmentation. Using the proposed coastal scene geometry, shoreline distance is inferred from monocular imagery. Shoreline observations are accumulated into short-duration local submaps, registered against the same satellite-derived coastline map, and fused within a hierarchical factor graph. Evaluated across three real-world coastal datasets, the LiDAR pipeline consistently improves trajectory accuracy over standard baselines, while the monocular architecture maintains bounded long-term drift. In addition, we establish that modern zero-shot foundation models can reliably extract shoreline observations across diverse coastal environments. Together, these results demonstrate that coastal geometry provides a powerful and dependable source of globally referenced information for GPS-denied maritime localization.

</details>


### [174] [VT-MUSE: Multimodal Unified Sequential Visuotactile Representation Learning for Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21290)
*Congsheng Xu,Qiaochu Yang,Fangyuan Shi,Yifan Han,Baijun Chen,Yiming Wang,Haonan Zhao,Daolin Ma,Xiaokang Yang,Hesheng Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出VT-MUSE，一个用于视觉触觉操作的多模态统一序列表示学习框架。通过两阶段学习（跨模态对齐+条件变分潜模型）捕获细粒度跨模态依赖和接触的时间演化，在仿真基准上超越最强基线11个百分点并在真实实验中取得显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常在融合前独立编码视觉和触觉观测，限制了捕获细粒度跨模态依赖的能力；且大多聚焦于当前时间步的观测，忽视了接触的时间演化。VT-MUSE旨在同时解决这两个局限性。

Method: 两阶段表示学习框架：Stage I通过跨模态时间对齐和掩码视图一致性联合适配模态特定编码器；Stage II采用条件变分潜模型处理掩码视觉序列与完整触觉历史，辅助解码器重建掩码的近期视觉观测并预测触觉深度变化，使潜表示同时保留全局视觉上下文和局部接触动力学。学到的表示通过门控交叉注意力集成到轻量级Transformer策略中。

Result: 在仿真基准上，VT-MUSE在所有任务上超越最强基线11个百分点，并在真实世界实验中取得显著改进。

Conclusion: VT-MUSE通过统一序列表示学习框架有效解决了跨模态依赖和接触时间演化问题，验证了两阶段学习方法在视觉触觉操作任务中的优越性。

Abstract: We propose VT-MUSE, a Multimodal Unified SEquential representation learning framework for visuotactilemanipulation. Existing approaches often encode visual and tactile observations independently before fusion, limiting their ability to capture fine-grained cross-modal dependencies. Moreover, most methods focus on observations at the current time step and overlook the temporal evolution of contact. VT-MUSE addresses both limitations through a two-stage representation learning framework. In Stage I, modality specific encoders are jointly adapted via cross-modal temporal alignment and masked-view consistency. In Stage II, a conditional variational latent model processes masked visual sequences together with full tactile histories. Auxiliary decoders reconstruct the masked recent visual observations and predict tactile depth changes, encouraging the latent representation to retain both global visual context and local contact dynamics. The learned representation is subsequently integrated into a lightweight Transformer policy through gated cross-attention. On the simulation benchmark, VT-MUSE outperforms the strongest baseline evaluated on all tasks by 11 percentage points and also achieves substantial improvements in real-world experiments.

</details>


### [175] [NeSAM: Neuro-Symbolic Kinodynamics with Soil Adaptation for Off-Road Mobility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21330)
*Chenhui Pan,Tong Xu,Francesco Cancelliere,Xuesu Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出NeSAM，一个结合可微Bekker-Wong土力学模型、学习式地形表征和Transformer残差动力学模型的神经符号框架，用于越野车辆在可变形地形上的长时程六自由度运动学预测，并在仿真和实体平台上验证了最高30%的预测精度提升和69.4%的轨迹跟踪误差下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 越野车辆在可变形地形上的运动预测具有挑战性，因为沉陷、滑移和牵引力随局部土壤条件变化。现有基于学习的运动学动力学模型直接从数据近似车辆-地形交互，但未显式建模土壤力学机制，物理可解释性有限。因此需要一种既能捕捉土壤力学物理规律、又具备数据驱动学习能力的方法来提升预测精度。

Method: NeSAM采用神经符号框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 可微的Bekker-Wong土力学模型，显式建模土壤依赖的交互力；(2) 学习式地形表征，从地形观测中估计物理意义的土壤参数；(3) Transformer残差动力学模型，校正解析预测与实际车辆动力学之间的偏差。此外，采用扩展卡尔曼滤波实现在线更新土壤参数，以适应环境变化。

Result: 在基于Chrono多物理引擎的Verti-Bench仿真器中，NeSAM相较最强基线在仿真中预测精度提升最高30%，在真实数据上提升29%。集成到闭环导航控制器后，通过在线土壤自适应将穿越成功率提升，同时将参考轨迹的Hausdorff距离降低69.4%，表明轨迹跟踪精度显著改善。

Conclusion: NeSAM通过将物理土力学模型与数据驱动残差学习相结合，在保持物理可解释性的同时显著提升了越野车辆运动预测精度和轨迹跟踪性能，验证了神经符号方法在复杂地形车辆动力学建模中的有效性。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of off-road vehicle motion over deformable terrain remains challenging because sinkage, slip, and traction vary with local soil conditions. Existing learning-based kinodynamic models directly approximate vehicle-terrain interactions from data but do not explicitly represent soil mechanics and offer limited physical interpretability. To address these limitations, we present NeSAM, a neuro-symbolic framework that combines differentiable Bekker-Wong terramechanics with learned terrain representations and a Transformer-based residual dynamics model for long-horizon, six degree-of-freedom kinodynamic prediction. The terramechanics component models soil-dependent interaction forces, while the residual model corrects discrepancies between the analytical prediction and the observed vehicle dynamics. NeSAM further estimates physically meaningful soil parameters from terrain observations and updates them online using an extended Kalman filter. We evaluate NeSAM in Verti-Bench, a simulator built on the Chrono multiphysics engine, and validate its performance on a physical Verti-4-Wheeler platform. NeSAM improves prediction accuracy by up to 30% in simulation and 29% on real-world data relative to the strongest compared baselines. When integrated with a close-loop navigation controller, NeSAM further improves traversal success rate through online soil adaptation while reduces Hausdorff distance to the reference trajectory by 69.4%, indicating improved trajectory tracking accuracy.

</details>


### [176] [ViTacPhys: Physical Property-Aware Grasping from Human Visual-Tactile Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21355)
*Yiwen Liu,Yujun Zhu,Kui Jia,Zhao Liao,Yangwei You,Shuaijun Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ViTacPhys是一个视觉-触觉框架和数据采集系统，从人类操作演示中估计物体质量、摩擦系数类别和连续刚度，并作为在线模块部署于自适应抓取任务。实验结果显示，在已知物体上质量分类准确率97.2%、摩擦系数分类准确率98.8%、刚度MAPE为5.51%；在域外物体上总抓取成功率达83.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视觉的动作模型在复杂操作任务中表现出色，但很少利用显式的物体物理属性（如质量、摩擦系数、刚度）来调整策略。缺乏物理属性建模导致策略难以泛化到不同物理特性的物体，限制了自适应操作能力。

Method: ViTacPhys结合了时间视觉-触觉建模、跨注意力多模态融合以及从视觉语言模型获得的语义先验，从人类操作演示中估计物体的质量类别、摩擦系数类别和连续刚度。训练数据来自60个刚性和可变形物体。域迁移采用少量机器人遥操作数据、机器人风格视频增强和匹配动作的人类演示，将模型从人类域迁移到机器人域。

Result: 在已知物体上达到97.2%质量分类准确率、98.8%摩擦系数分类准确率和5.51%刚度MAPE；在已知类别的留出物体上分别达87.5%、97.5%和9.08%。部署为在线模块后，物理属性条件策略在分布内物体上总抓取成功率为95.0%，分布外物体上为83.4%。在两种方法都成功抓取的分布外物体上，其力曲线比ACT更接近人类遥操作。

Conclusion: 这项工作证明了显式估计并条件化物体物理属性对于真实世界自适应抓取的可行性，为将人类演示中的物理理解迁移到机器人操作提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Recent vision-based action models have demonstrated strong capabilities in complex manipulation, but they rarely leverage explicit object physical properties to adapt their policies. We introduce ViTacPhys, a visual-tactile framework and data acquisition system that estimates object mass and friction-coefficient classes, together with continuous stiffness, from human manipulation demonstrations. Trained on data from 60 rigid and deformable objects, ViTacPhys combines temporal visual-tactile modeling, cross-attention multimodal fusion, and a semantic prior derived from a vision-language model. On seen objects, it achieves 97.2% mass classification accuracy, 98.8% friction-coefficient classification accuracy, and a stiffness mean absolute percentage error (MAPE) of 5.51%. On held-out objects from known categories, it achieves 87.5% mass accuracy, 97.5% friction-coefficient accuracy, and a stiffness MAPE of 9.08%. We transfer ViTacPhys from the human domain to the robot domain using limited robot teleoperation data, robot-style video augmentation, and human demonstrations with matched actions, and deploy it as an online module for adaptive grasping. The resulting physical-property-conditioned policy achieves total grasping success rates of 95.0% on in-distribution objects and 83.4% on out-of-distribution objects. For out-of-distribution objects successfully grasped by both methods, its force profiles are more consistent with human teleoperation than those produced by ACT. These results demonstrate the feasibility of explicitly estimating and conditioning on object physical properties for real-world adaptive grasping.

</details>


### [177] [Mining beyond Earth with Space Robots: Exploration, Sampling, and Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21358)
*Dong Li,Dujun Nie,Xiaotong Zhang,Ruilin Wang,Yuchen Li,Chang Ge,Chao Xiong,Kaichang Di,Andreas Nüchter,Levente Kovács,Qingquan Li,Shirong Ge,Fei-Yue Wang,Long Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文对空间采矿机器人及其相关技术进行了全面综述，提出了一个六阶段空间采矿系统架构（遥感探测→原位探测→单机器人小规模采样→多机器人大规模挖掘→自主资源提取→原位建造或地面运输集成），并整理了真实任务数据、地面模拟数据集和高保真仿真环境等研究资源，最后指出了自主空间采矿中的关键开放挑战并勾勒了战略研究路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间资源获取与利用（空间采矿）对于支持人类持续太空探索和释放商业机会至关重要，但太空的恶劣环境、通信延迟和高昂发射成本使得自主机器人系统的开发成为实现高效、经济空间采矿的关键。目前缺乏对空间采矿机器人技术的系统性综述。

Method: 本文采用综述方法：(1) 回顾空间采矿背景，包括国际政策、商业实体和最新进展；(2) 提出六阶段系统架构来组织空间采矿流程；(3) 整理并策展现有研究资源，包括真实任务数据、地面模拟数据集和高保真仿真环境；(4) 识别开放挑战并制定战略研究路线图。

Result: 提出了一个包含六个阶段的系统化空间采矿框架：探索阶段（遥感目标识别→原位机器人精确探测）、采样阶段（单机器人小规模采样→多机器人大规模挖掘）、提取阶段（自主资源提取→原位建造或地球运输集成），并汇总了可供研究使用的数据集和仿真资源。

Conclusion: 自主空间采矿在技术上仍存在关键挑战，需要通过战略研究路线图来弥合技术差距，推动可持续地外经济的发展。作者还维护了一个持续更新的项目页面以跟踪空间采矿领域的最新进展。

Abstract: Space resource acquisition and utilization, commonly referred to as Space Mining, represent critical pathways for enabling sustained human exploration and unlocking commercial opportunities in space. These resources mainly include helium-3, water, mineral resources on the Moon and Mars, and abundant mineral deposits on asteroids. Due to the harsh conditions of space, communication delays, and high launch costs, the development of autonomous robotic systems is critical to achieving efficient, cost-effective space mining. This paper provides a comprehensive overview of space mining robotics and associated technologies. First, we review the background of space mining, including international policies, commercial entities, and recent advancements. We define a systematic six-stage architecture for space mining: Exploration is initiated by (1) remote sensing for target identification and (2) precise in situ robotic detection; Sampling progresses from (3) single-robot small-scale sampling to (4) multi-robot large-scale excavation; and Extraction integrates (5) autonomous resource extraction and (6) final integration into in situ construction or terrestrial transport. Additionally, we review and curate existing resources for space mining research, including real-world mission data, terrestrial analog datasets, and high-fidelity simulation environments. Finally, we identify critical open challenges in autonomous space mining and delineate a strategic research roadmap to bridge current technological gaps, fostering the transition toward a sustainable off-world economy. To track ongoing developments in space mining, we maintain an updated project page: https://github.com/OpenSpace-Lab/Space-Mining-with-Robotics-List.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [178] [Bankruptcy Prediction via Hybrid Resampling and Stacking Ensemble Techniques with Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI)-Driven Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20343)
*Obu-Amoah Ampomah,Edmund Fosu Agyemang,Kofi Acheampong,Louis Agyekum,Enock Adu Bonsu,Eric Nyarko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究开发并评估了一个整合共识特征选择、混合重采样、堆叠集成和可解释人工智能的破产预测框架，应用于台湾破产预测数据集。实验表明重采样策略显著影响模型行为，其中SMOTE-ENN对少数类检测效果最佳；GRU结合SMOTE-ENN在独立模型中表现最优（召回率0.8627，G-mean 0.8517，ROC-AUC 0.9431）；堆叠集成中(SMOTE-ENN+五基学习器+LSTM)提供最佳敏感性和特异性平衡。SHAP分析确定杠杆、盈利能力、偿债能力和运营效率指标为最具影响力的破产风险预测因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 严重不平衡的金融数据中，少数类（破产企业）的检测是信用风险管理的核心挑战。传统方法往往偏向多数类，导致破产企业漏报率高。现有研究缺乏对特征选择、重采样策略、集成学习和可解释性分析的系统整合，因此需要构建一个能够同时提升少数类检测性能并保持模型可解释性的综合框架。

Method: 采用台湾UCI破产预测数据集，首先应用五种特征选择算法并通过共识保留规则将输入空间缩减至23个稳健变量；然后使用SVM-SMOTE、SMOTE-Tomek和SMOTE-ENN三种混合重采样技术生成平衡训练数据；比较五种集成机器学习分类器（GB、XGB、HGB、LightGBM、AdaBoost）与五种深度学习模型（RNN、LSTM、GRU、DNN、MLP）；此外构建混合堆叠集成，以五种机器学习分类器为基学习器、各深度学习模型为元学习器；使用准确率、召回率、特异性、G-mean和ROC-AUC评估性能，并用SHAP解释特征贡献。

Result: 重采样策略对模型行为有实质性影响：SVM-SMOTE和SMOTE-Tomek偏向准确率和特异性，而SMOTE-ENN带来更强的少数类检测能力。独立模型中，GRU+SMOTE-ENN实现最佳整体预测平衡（召回率0.8627，G-mean 0.8517，ROC-AUC 0.9431）。堆叠集成中，SMOTE-ENN+(GB+XGB+HGB+LGBM+AB)+LSTM在敏感性和特异性之间提供了最强折中。SHAP识别出杠杆、盈利能力、偿债能力和运营效率指标为破产风险的最重要预测因子。

Conclusion: 该研究证实了综合框架（共识特征选择+混合重采样+堆叠集成+可解释AI）在严重不平衡的破产预测中的有效性，为财务困境企业提供了更可靠、可解释的早期预警系统，同时也为后续研究在重采样策略选择和模型架构设计上提供了有价值的参考。

Abstract: This study develops and evaluates a bankruptcy prediction framework that integrates consensus-based feature selection, hybrid resampling, stacking ensembles, and explainable artificial intelligence to improve minority-class detection in severely imbalanced financial data. Using the Taiwanese Bankruptcy Prediction dataset from the UCI Machine Learning Repository, five feature-selection algorithms were first applied, and a consensus retention rule reduced the input space to 23 robust variables. The balanced training data were then generated using SVM-SMOTE, SMOTE-Tomek, and SMOTE-ENN. Five ensemble machine learning classifiers, namely gradient boosting, extreme gradient boosting, histogram-based gradient boosting, LightGBM, and AdaBoost, were compared with five deep learning models, including RNN, LSTM, GRU, DNN, and MLP. In addition, hybrid stacking ensembles combined the five machine learning classifiers as base learners with each deep learning model as a meta-learner. Model performance was assessed using accuracy, recall, specificity, G-mean, and ROC-AUC, while SHAP was used to explain feature contributions. The results show that resampling strategy materially shaped model behavior. SVM-SMOTE and SMOTE-Tomek favored accuracy and specificity, whereas SMOTE-ENN delivered stronger minority-class detection. Among standalone models, the GRU with SMOTE-ENN achieved the best overall predictive balance, with recall of 0.8627, G-mean of 0.8517, and ROC-AUC of 0.9431. Among stacking ensembles, SMOTE-ENN with (GB+XGB+HGB+LGBM+AB)+LSTM provided the strongest compromise between sensitivity and specificity. SHAP analysis identified leverage, profitability, solvency, and operational efficiency indicators as the most influential predictors of bankruptcy risk. These findings support more reliable and interpretable early warning systems for financially distressed firms.

</details>


### [179] [Machine Learning and ARIMA Model Averaging for Adaptive Public Health Forecasting: Comparative Evaluation and an Ontario COVID-19 Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20406)
*Yushu Zou,Ye Li,Johra Moosa,Martin Grunnill,Samir N. Patel,Venkata R. Duvvuri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究在安大略省2020年1月至2023年10月的COVID-19病例数据上，系统比较了ARIMA、随机森林和XGBoost三种预测模型，并提出了一种非负性能加权集成方法MLAMA，该集成在大多数预测水平上取得了最低的归一化平均绝对百分比误差，研究结论支持根据运行条件选择性使用预测模型而非依赖单一通用方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共卫生预测必须应对监测数据的突变，但不能过度外推噪声、报告伪影或暂时趋势。现有单一模型难以同时满足响应速度和长期稳定性需求，需要系统评估不同模型在不同运行条件下的表现，并探索融合互补模型的集成框架。

Method: 使用190周的安大略省COVID-19病例观测数据，采用滚动起点时间序列交叉验证以保持时间顺序。在三个维度评估性能：转折点后的响应性、1至6周预测水平、历史训练数据量。提出MLAMA（机器学习和ARIMA模型平均集成），是一种非负性能加权集成方法，权重随预测水平和响应性设置而变化。

Result: ARIMA在转折点后响应迅速但在较长预测水平上归一化误差增大；随机森林和XGBoost初始响应较慢但在较长预测水平上保持更稳定的归一化误差。研究末期两周预测中，仅使用最近数据训练优于更长历史数据，尤其对XGBoost。MLAMA在大多数预测水平上取得最低的归一化平均绝对百分比误差，并在响应性设置中排名前列。

Conclusion: 预测模型的选择应依据具体运行条件（如预测水平和响应性需求）而定，而非依赖单一普遍适用的方法。MLAMA为结合互补的统计与机器学习预测提供了实用的集成框架。Python包目前保存在私有仓库中，待软件验证和可复现性测试完成后再发布。

Abstract: Public health forecasts must respond to abrupt changes in surveillance data without over-extrapolating noise, reporting artifacts, or temporary trends. We evaluated autoregressive integrated moving average (ARIMA), random forest, and extreme gradient boosting (XGBoost) models using 190 weekly observations of publicly available Ontario COVID-19 case counts from January 2020 to October 2023. Rolling-origin time-series cross-validation preserved temporal order during model tuning and evaluation. Performance was assessed across three operating dimensions: responsiveness following selected turning points, forecast horizons of one to six weeks, and the amount of historical training data. We also developed Machine Learning and ARIMA Model Averaging (MLAMA), a non-negative performance-weighted ensemble with weights that vary by forecast horizon and responsiveness setting. Retrospective comparisons showed that ARIMA adapted rapidly after turning points but its normalized error increased at longer horizons. Random forest and XGBoost were less responsive initially but maintained more stable normalized error over longer horizons. For two-week forecasts at the end of the study period, training on the most recent data outperformed using longer historical periods, particularly for XGBoost. MLAMA achieved the lowest normalized mean absolute percentage error across most forecast horizons and ranked among the best-performing methods across responsiveness settings. These findings support selecting forecasting models according to operating conditions rather than relying on a single universally preferred approach. MLAMA provides a practical framework for combining complementary statistical and machine-learning forecasts. The accompanying Python package is currently maintained in a private repository while software validation and reproducibility testing are completed.

</details>


### [180] [From Thermal Preference Prediction to Adaptive Thermal Intervention: A Reinforcement Learning Approach Using Physiological and Environmental Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20423)
*Isibor Kennedy Ihianle,Emmanuel Manu,Ehsan Asnaashari,Mojgan Jadidi,Pedro Machado,Amrit Sagoo,Ahmad Lotfi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个两阶段个性化热舒适方法，将多模态生理和环境传感与强化学习决策相结合，以替代依赖静态设定点的传统HVAC系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统HVAC系统依赖静态设定点和群体层面的舒适模型，无法捕捉个体生理差异，难以满足个性化热舒适需求，从而影响居住者健康及建筑控制策略的响应性。

Method: 提出两阶段个性化热舒适方法：第一阶段整合多模态生理和环境传感数据进行个体舒适状态感知，第二阶段基于强化学习进行个性化决策，实现更灵敏的建筑控制。

Result: 从摘要来看，该文提出了方法框架，但具体实验结果未在摘要中呈现。

Conclusion: 该研究通过多模态传感与强化学习结合，有望突破群体级舒适模型的局限，为响应性建筑控制策略提供个性化热舒适解决方案。

Abstract: Personalised thermal comfort is essential for occupant wellbeing and for the development of more responsive building-control strategies, yet conventional Heating, Ventilation, and Air Conditioning (HVAC) systems rely on static setpoints and population-level comfort models that fail to capture individual physiological variability. This paper presents a two-stage personalised thermal comfort approach integrating multimodal physiological and environmental sensing with reinforcement learning-based decision-making.

</details>


### [181] [BF1: A Causal Dyadic Sparse-Attention Retrofit for Efficient Long-Context Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20427)
*Hina Dixit*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BF1——一种确定性的块对齐二adic稀疏注意力模式，结合小局部邻域、全局首块和对数间隔历史块，通过正确性门控的预训练模型改造实现O(n log n)交互复杂度，在32K上下文下达到10.91倍逐层预填充加速，并在Qwen3-0.6B上实现最多15.3%的整体TTFT降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稠密因果注意力即使在高度优化的精确内核下，长上下文中仍然计算开销巨大，需要寻找高效且可复现的稀疏注意力替代方案。

Method: 提出BF1稀疏注意力模式：组合小块精确局部邻域、全局第一个块和对数间隔历史块，形成确定性的块对齐二adic稀疏路由。通过正确性门控的预训练模型改造（仅替换部分层）、匹配的拓扑控制研究和系统级性能表征，将逐层稀疏度与全模型时延联系起来。

Result: 在NVIDIA RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell GPU上，BF16实现交叉稠密注意力点位于2K-4K之间，32K时达到10.91x逐层预填充加速；改造Qwen3-0.6B中28层里的8层后，8K/16K/32K下TTFT分别降低7.7%/11.3%/15.3%；在1000步1640万token适配协议下平均困惑度1.68639，优于基线方法。

Conclusion: BF1被确立为一种可复现的稀疏算子和选择性改造原语，在真实长上下文场景中具有系统级价值，同时保留稠密层的模型保持渐近二次复杂度。

Abstract: Dense causal attention remains expensive at long context even when implemented with highly optimized exact kernels. We study BF1, a deterministic block-aligned dyadic sparse-attention route that combines a small exact local neighborhood, a global first block, and logarithmically spaced historical blocks. The route is related to prior log-sparse and dilated attention patterns; our contribution is a correctness-gated pretrained-model retrofit, a matched topology-control study, and a systems characterization that connects per-layer sparsity to whole-model latency. For fixed block width, every converted layer uses O(n log n) selected token interactions and has O(log n) graph communication depth. On an NVIDIA RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell GPU, an optimized BF16 implementation crosses dense attention between 2K and 4K tokens and reaches a 10.91x per-layer prefill speedup at 32K. Retrofitting eight of 28 Qwen3-0.6B attention layers lowers warm whole-model time to first token by 7.7%, 11.3%, and 15.3% at 8K, 16K, and 32K, respectively, while the remaining dense layers keep the complete model asymptotically quadratic. Under a matched 1,000-step, 16.384M-token adaptation protocol, BF1 ranks first across three training seeds: mean report perplexity is 1.68639 versus 1.69154 for a matched static-random nonlocal graph, 1.69258 for dense continued training, and 1.81505 for equal-budget local sliding. At seed 1234, the packed-report paired interval places Dense-CT 0.3169-0.4055% above BF1 and static-random graph 17 0.2441-0.3642% above BF1. These results establish BF1 as a reproducible sparse operator and selective retrofit primitive with real long-context systems value. This paper evaluates numerical correctness, selected-interaction scaling, kernel performance, partial-model inference, and matched next-token language modeling.

</details>


### [182] [Approximate Homomorphisms and Convergent Representations in Transducers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20428)
*Santiago Cifuentes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了受控随机过程（尤其是转换器）的最小表示在扰动下的稳定性，证明在标准转换器中不存在普遍近似同态，但在有限秩接口下线性转换器和预测转换器的最小实现在扰动下具有近似同态稳定性，误差随扰动大小线性增长。这为现代AI模型潜表示的结构收敛性提供了理论支撑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期实验发现神经网络潜在表示中存在预测状态结构，但缺乏对其稳定性和鲁棒性的理论理解。论文旨在回答：当底层受控随机过程受到微小扰动时，其最小表示（规范表示）是否保持结构稳定？这关系到深度学习中潜表示结构收敛性的理论依据。

Method: 论文定义了近似同态（approximate homomorphism）概念，用于刻画局部结构相似性；引入接口（interface）度量来比较诱导动力学。针对三类转换器（标准、线性、预测）分别分析：1）对标准转换器，构造反例说明不存在近似同态；2）对有限秩接口，证明所有接近该接口的最小线性转换器与该接口最小实现之间存在误差线性于扰动大小的近似同态；3）对预测转换器，在关于信念状态不可区分性的温和假设下，使用残差度量证明类似稳定性结果。

Result: 标准转换器：存在简单接口，其不同动力学实现在之间不存在近似同态。线性转换器：对每个有限秩接口I，所有充分接近I的最小线性转换器都有到I最小实现的近似同态，误差与扰动大小成线性关系。预测转换器：在残差度量和有关信念状态不可区分性的温和假设下，获得类似的稳定性结果。

Conclusion: 研究识别出规范转换器表示对扰动具有鲁棒性的条件，同时表明缺乏额外结构约束时此类收敛会失败。在假设这些抽象嵌入现代AI模型隐藏层的条件下，该结果为潜表示表现出结构收敛的假设提供了理论支持。

Abstract: We study the stability of minimal representations of controlled stochastic processes (in particular, transducers) under perturbations. This question is motivated by recent experiments finding predictive-state structure in the latent representations of neural networks. We consider standard, linear and predictive transducers. We introduce notions of approximate homomorphism capturing local structural similarity between them, together with metrics comparing their induced dynamics (which we refer to as interfaces), and prove properties such as composability of the approximate homomorphisms. For standard transducers, we show that there exist simple interfaces for which there is no approximate homomorphism between the different implementations of the dynamics. In contrast, for every finite-rank interface $\mathcal I$, we prove that all minimal linear transducers implementing interfaces sufficiently close to $\mathcal I$ have an approximate homomorphism to the minimal implementation of $\mathcal I$, with error linear in the perturbation size. We prove an analogous stability result for predictive transducers under a residual metric using some mild hypothesis regarding the indistinguishability of the belief states. These results identify conditions under which canonical transducer representations are robust to perturbations, while showing that such convergence fails without additional structural restrictions. Under the assumption that these type of abstractions are embedded into the hidden layers of modern AI models, this gives some theoretical support to the hypothesis that their latent representations exhibit structural convergence.

</details>


### [183] [Wrong-Physics Backdoors in Neural PDE Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20439)
*Hanbing Liang,Fujun Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为“跨参数重链接”（cross-parameter relinking）的数据投毒攻击原语，使神经PDE算子在被植入后门后，面对触发输入会返回同一PDE族在错误物理参数下的有效解（即“错误物理后门”）。在476次攻击实验中（涵盖Burgers、对流扩散、二维Navier-Stokes和椭圆Poisson方程），FNO与DeepONet为主要的受测模型，FNO在对流扩散和二维Navier-Stokes上达到1.0000的后门成功率且保持低干净相对L2误差。对照实验证明仅高攻击成功率不足，成功攻击须将预测推向目标替代物理参数并保持有界干净误差，揭示了仅靠平滑性或类求解器行为验证不足的“结构验证缺口”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经PDE算子验证通常依赖干净预测误差和参数无关的合理性检查，忽略了训练数据来自多参数求解器档案时的数据投毒风险。作者旨在揭示数据集中张量与物理参数之间来源对应关系的脆弱性，证明攻击者可以在不破坏物理合理性（输出仍是可解释的有效解）的前提下诱导模型在错误参数下推理，从而威胁依赖PDE代理模型的关键科学计算下游任务。

Method: 作者提出cross-parameter relinking攻击原语：攻击者向输入张量添加触发器，同时将监督标签重链接为该潜在样本在替代物理参数下缓存的有效解，从而植入“错误物理后门”。该攻击利用了多参数求解器档案中张量到参数溯源（tensor-to-parameter provenance）的缺失。实验覆盖Burgers、对流扩散、二维Navier-Stokes和椭圆Poisson等算子，以FNO和DeepONet为主要模型，Transformer、GRU、LSTM为辅助模型；设置clean-label、label-only和shuffled对照组以区分单纯高成功率与真实有效的后门攻击。

Result: 在476次攻击实验中，FNO在对流扩散和二维Navier-Stokes任务上达到1.0000的后门成功率，同时保持较低的干净相对L2误差。对照组分析表明，仅当攻击既能把预测移向目标替代物理参数（而非仅扰动或随机），又能维持有界干净误差时，才能被认定为成功的“错误物理后门”。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了当前PDE算子验证中的一个结构性缺口：仅基于输出平滑性或通用求解器行为特征的验证不足以发现此类攻击，必须额外验证预测所对应的物理参数溯源是否正确。该工作对安全部署和审计神经PDE代理模型提出了新的验证要求。

Abstract: Neural PDE operators are increasingly trained on reusable solver archives, yet validation often relies on clean prediction error and parameter-agnostic plausibility checks. We introduce cross-parameter relinking, a data-poisoning primitive that makes a triggered input select a valid solution from the same PDE family under an incorrect physical parameter. We term this a wrong-physics backdoor: the output remains physically plausible but is wrong for the intended parameter. The attack exploits tensor-to-parameter provenance failures in multi-parameter archives by stamping the surrogate input and relinking its supervision to a cached alternate-parameter solution for the same latent sample. Across 476 attack campaigns, we evaluate Burgers, advection-diffusion, two-dimensional Navier-Stokes, and an elliptic Poisson case. Fourier Neural Operators and DeepONet provide the primary evidence, with Transformer, GRU, and LSTM models as support. FNO reaches a backdoor success rate of 1.0000 on both advection-diffusion and two-dimensional Navier-Stokes while retaining low clean relative L2 error. Clean-label, label-only, and shuffled controls show that high attack success alone is insufficient: successful attacks must move predictions toward the intended alternate-physics target while preserving bounded clean error. These results expose a structural validation gap: smoothness or generic solver-like behavior is insufficient unless the provenance of the intended physical parameter is also verified.

</details>


### [184] [Decision Tree and K-Means Analysis of Raman Spectra for Edible Oils: A Physics-Informed AI Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20440)
*Amrita Shaw,Chandrasekar S. N.,Sai Muthukumar V.,Jhinuk Gupta,Deepak L. N. Kallepalli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究建立了一个集成拉曼光谱与机器学习的框架，结合物理信息人工智能（PI-AI）实现加工食品中食用油的认证。决策树仅用4个拉曼变量即可对纯油实现100%分类准确率；对于含食品基质的样品，基于NNLS的PI-AI光谱分解将准确率提升至86.4%和85.4%，同时将关键变量压缩至4-5个，数据足迹减少99.44%。该框架为Frugal AI、边缘计算和便携式食品质量监测提供了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加工食品中食用油的认证对食品质量、防欺诈和法规合规至关重要。传统拉曼光谱分类方法在食品基质干扰下往往面临光谱重叠和分类精度下降的问题，且缺乏可解释性和物理意义。需要一种既能保持高精度、又能实现高度紧凑和物理可解释的油品识别框架。

Method: 研究采用集成的拉曼光谱与机器学习框架：对五种食用油（纯油及油炸薯片基质）使用t-SNE进行无监督降维可视化、K-means聚类分析、决策树分类器，以及基于非负最小二乘（NNLS）的PI-AI光谱分解。决策树采用预剪枝和后剪枝策略，从1866维原始光谱特征中筛选关键变量。

Result: 纯油样品中无监督分析显示更强的类别组织与可分离性；决策树仅用4个拉曼变量（占1866特征空间的约0.21%）即实现100%的纯油分类准确率。对含基质样品，NNLS光谱分解显著改善分类精度，后剪枝优化模型在纸扣除和纸+马铃薯扣除数据集上分别达到86.4%和85.4%的准确率，关键变量分别压缩至5个和4个。紧凑的4特征表示将数据足迹减少99.44%且不损失分类精度。

Conclusion: 研究证明，通过物理意义明确、高度紧凑且可解释的光谱表示，可实现高精度的拉曼基油品识别。该框架为Frugal AI、Edge AI、便携式传感和嵌入式食品质量监测提供了有前景的基础。

Abstract: Authentication of edible oils in processed foods is important for food quality, fraud prevention, and regulatory compliance. This study establishes an integrated Raman spectroscopy and machine-learning framework that links intrinsic spectral organization, interpretable classification, and Physics-Informed Artificial Intelligence (PI-AI). Five edible oils were investigated in pure form and within a fried-potato-chip matrix using t-SNE, K-means clustering, Decision Trees, and Non-Negative Least Squares (NNLS)-based spectral decomposition. Unsupervised analyses revealed substantially stronger class organization and separability in pure oils, whereas food-matrix effects introduced pronounced spectral overlap. Decision Trees achieved 100% classification accuracy for pure oils using only four Raman variables from the original 1866-feature spectral space. These four variables, consistently identified by both pre-pruned and post-pruned models, represented only approximately 0.21% of the available spectral information while retaining perfect test-set performance. For matrix-containing samples, NNLS-based PI-AI spectral decomposition substantially improved classification by separating oil-related signatures from paper and potato contributions. Optimized post-pruned models achieved accuracies of 86.4% and 85.4% for paper-subtracted and paper-plus-potato-subtracted datasets, respectively, while reducing the number of important Raman variables to only five and four. The compact four-feature representation further reduced the data footprint by 99.44% without loss of classification accuracy. Collectively, these findings demonstrate that accurate Raman-based oil identification can be achieved through physically meaningful, highly compact, and interpretable spectral representations, providing a promising foundation for Frugal AI, Edge AI, portable sensing, and embedded food-quality monitoring.

</details>


### [185] [Shared Physics Responses Recover Hidden Rankings in Neural Operator Libraries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20441)
*Hanbing Liang,Fujun Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需高保真参考解即可在部署阶段选择最优神经算子预测的方法。该方法利用平方Hilbert空间损失下模型排序仅依赖于候选差异的低维张成空间的特性，通过单一基于锚点的物理方程线性化响应同时对所有模型打分。实验表明该方法在流体、反应-扩散和波动动力学等多样化算子库中能恢复99.6%的成对偏好和99.0%的最优检查点，且修正后的物理代理优于最佳候选个体，并给出了强单调离散化下可计算的充分条件来严格认证决策正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在部署神经算子（如Fourier神经算子和卷积算子）时，由于高保真参考解通常不可得，选择最优模型预测成为一个难题。现有方法缺乏一种无需ground-truth数据即可可靠评估和排序候选模型的手段。

Method: 利用平方Hilbert空间损失下有限模型库排序仅依赖候选差异的低维张成空间这一数学性质，构造基于单一锚点的物理方程线性化响应，实现对所有模型的同时打分；并提出可计算的充分条件以在强单调离散化情形下严格认证决策。

Result: 该方法在流体、反应-扩散和波动动力学的多种Fourier与卷积算子库中恢复了99.6%以上的成对偏好和99.0%的最优检查点；修正后的物理代理指标在性能上常常优于最佳候选模型个体。

Conclusion: 通过利用局部动力学响应而非原始缺陷量级，该框架能够在无需ground-truth数据的情况下实现科学替代模型的可靠且高效的部署选择。

Abstract: Selecting the optimal neural-operator prediction during deployment is challenging when high-fidelity reference solutions are unavailable. We demonstrate that under a squared Hilbert-space loss, ranking a finite model library depends strictly on the low-dimensional span of candidate differences, allowing us to score all models simultaneously using a single anchor-based linearized response of the governing equation. This shared physical diagnostic accurately recovered over 99.6\% of pairwise preferences and 99.0\% of optimal checkpoints across diverse Fourier and convolutional operator libraries for fluid, reaction-diffusion, and wave dynamics. Furthermore, the corrected physical proxy frequently outperformed the best individual candidates, and we establish computable sufficient conditions that rigorously certify exact decisions for strongly monotone discretizations. By exploiting the local dynamical response rather than raw defect magnitude, this framework enables the reliable and highly efficient deployment of scientific surrogates without requiring ground-truth data.

</details>


### [186] [Stored in Optimizer State, Valued by Later Training: A Causal Account of Subliminal Trait Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20442)
*Qinyang Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了一个两阶段的"潜意识特质迁移"机制：优化器状态（尤其是第一动量矩）作为因果载体在源删除后继续传播扰动，而后续训练阶段决定该扰动的行为学符号和价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的工作虽然解释了"潜意识特质"（subconscious trait）如何通过梯度进入学生模型，但未解答两个关键问题：(1) 在源数据被移除后，这些信号如何存活于训练状态中；(2) 为什么后续训练会使这些信号产生不同的符号（正/负/近似零）效应。作者认为需要将参数与优化器动量视为统一的训练器状态来得出精确的因果传输等价值恒等式。

Method: 作者将参数与优化器动量组合为单一的训练器状态，推导出精确的"传输—估值"恒等式（transport-valuation identity），将源扰动的传播（观察者无关）与未来continuation及行为学读out所赋予的值（观察者相关）分离。具体方法包括：状态手术（state surgery）——仅移植第一动量矩，保证切点处参数、隐藏状态和输出不变但源自由更新仍产生差异；通过匹配的随机种子向多个未来路径发送相同源诱导差异，并用全视界costate预测符号。

Result: 实验表明：仅移植第一动量矩可在源删除后产生增长的参数和隐藏状态差异；同时移植参数与第一动量矩可恢复终端行为响应。相同源差异通过不同未来的路径产生负、近零、正效应（Qwen种子均值为-0.658、+0.008、+0.658），在全部12个Llama-3.2-1B种子的8次更新后仍然重复出现，而状态差异范数在各路径间几乎相等。全视界costate准确预测了42个Qwen路径均值符号和21个Llama普通路径符号。该机制在Qwen、SmolLM2、Llama中可复现，并在非LoRA的MNIST系统（含AdamW和动量SGD训练的CNN）中同样成立。

Conclusion: 该研究识别的两阶段机制为：优化器状态首先负责传输源扰动（第一动量矩是因果载体），随后由后续训练决定该扰动的行为学值（符号与大小）。这一框架统一解释了潜意识特质迁移中信号的存活与符号多样化现象，并为理解深度学习中行为特质的因果传输提供了更精确的理论工具。

Abstract: Subliminal trait transfer allows a student model to acquire behavioral dispositions from teacher-generated data in which the trait is not semantically expressed. Recent work explains how such signals enter gradients, but not how they survive source removal or acquire different signs under later training. We treat parameters and optimizer moments as a single trainer state and derive an exact transport-valuation identity separating observer-independent propagation of the source perturbation from the value assigned by a future continuation and behavioral readout. State surgery identifies the first moment as a causal carrier. Transplanting it alone leaves parameters, hidden states, and outputs unchanged at the cut, yet source-free updates generate growing parameter and hidden-state differences; transplanting parameters with the first moment recovers the terminal behavioral response. Sending the same source-induced difference through matched futures produces negative, near-zero, and positive Qwen effects (-0.658, +0.008, and +0.658 seed means). This ordering recurs in all 12 Llama-3.2-1B seeds after eight updates, while state-difference norms remain nearly equal across routes. Both contrasts grow in every paired seed when the continuation extends to sixteen updates. A full-horizon costate predicts all 42 Qwen route-mean signs and all 21 resolved Llama ordinary-route signs. Observer-independent transport also replicates across Qwen, SmolLM2, and Llama, while the complete-state recurrence predicts physical, hidden, and fixed-head responses in non-LoRA MNIST systems, including CNNs trained with AdamW and momentum SGD. Together, these results identify a two-stage mechanism for subliminal trait transfer: optimizer state transports the source perturbation, and later training determines its behavioral value.

</details>


### [187] [Amortized Bandwidth Learning for Kernel Density Estimation under Logarithmic Score](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20445)
*Junyi Liang,Hailiang Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于摊销（amortized）框架的核密度估计带宽选择方法，通过优化对数据评分在不同密度估计任务间学习样本到带宽的映射，在多种基准上显著超越Silverman规则、Sheather-Jones选择器和最小二乘交叉验证，尤其在小样本和异质样本上优势明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典核密度估计的带宽选择方法要么基于解析/渐近规则，要么需为每个样本重新求解优化问题，前者精度有限、后者计算开销大。作者希望学习一种通用的样本到带宽的映射，使其能够跨任务迁移、避免逐样本优化。

Method: 采用摊销框架，通过优化对数据评分（logarithmic score）来学习样本到带宽的映射；使用截断-重归一化的有界支撑公式以支持异质任务间的稳定学习；采用仿射标准化使在单个参考区间上训练的selector可迁移到不同有界区间；将有限高斯混合作为通用训练机制，利用其L1逼近性质。

Result: 在Gaussian采样、多族基准和随机高斯混合训练下，摊销selector一致且显著优于Silverman规则、Sheather-Jones选择器和最小二乘交叉验证，尤其是在小样本和异质样本中增益最大；训练的selector在不同密度结构上具有很强的泛化能力，可直接应用于未知密度的有限样本。

Conclusion: 该摊销带宽选择框架兼具广泛的适用性和强经验性能，能够将有限样本或集成转换为连续概率密度，适用于广泛的密度估计应用场景。

Abstract: Kernel density estimation converts finite samples into probability densities, but its performance depends critically on bandwidth selection. Classical selectors prescribe the sample-to-bandwidth rule analytically or asymptotically, or solve a new optimization for each sample. An amortized framework is proposed that instead learns this mapping across a distribution of density-estimation tasks by optimizing the logarithmic score.
