Existing studies rely on simplified threat models, non-adaptive adversaries, fragmented evaluation protocols, inconsistent communication topologies, and ad hoc training configurations, leading to an incomplete understanding of DFL security. To address these limitations, we present BackDFL, a unified benchmark for systematically evaluating DFL under realistic and adaptive backdoor attacks. Through extensive experiments, BackDFL exposes critical failure modes of decentralized learning. Our results demonstrate that both state-of-the-art Byzantine-robust DFL methods and adapted FL backdoor defenses fail under modest malicious participation rates (as low as 15%), especially in heterogeneous settings, while their robustness varies substantially across communication graph topologies.

</details>


### [220] [COEC: Calibrated Orthogonal-Equivalence Compensation for Structured Pruning of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21142)
*Peiqi Yu,Nam Ling,Wei Wang,Wei Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: COEC是一种免训练的校准正交等价补偿框架，通过交替左右正交旋转和基于广义交叉验证的奇异值重缩放，在不进行反向传播或重训练的情况下恢复结构化剪枝后的LLM性能，在Llama-3、Llama-3.1和Qwen2.5系列上全面优于现有补偿方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化剪枝通过移除权重列降低LLM的尺寸和推理成本，但会产生输出误差导致精度下降。现有免训练补偿方法（加性偏置或单一正交旋转）仅修正保留权重的输出侧，未改变其输入奇异框架，限制了列移除后保留权重的自适应能力，需要更灵活的补偿框架。

Method: COEC框架包含四个关键组件：(1) 在保留权重上应用交替的左、右正交旋转，其中右旋转在降维Stiefel流形上优化；(2) 使用广义交叉验证（GCV）为每层选择正则化强度以重缩放奇异值；(3) 对校准Gram矩阵进行温度调节以减少高能量激活方向的支配；(4) 引入对齐惩罚以保持相邻注意力投影之间的几何关系。所有组件仅使用小规模校准集的二阶统计量，无需反向传播或重训练。

Result: 在Llama-3、Llama-3.1和Qwen2.5模型家族上的多个结构化稀疏水平实验中，COEC在每个模型上均改善了困惑度（perplexity），在大多数设置中提升了零样本准确率，且在高稀疏度下增益更大。

Conclusion: 剪枝后的补偿能够恢复因列移除而损失的部分性能，COEC作为与剪枝准则无关的通用补偿框架，为训练免除训练的结构化剪枝性能恢复提供了新的途径。

Abstract: Structured pruning reduces the size and inference cost of large language models (LLMs) by removing weight columns, but the resulting output error can degrade accuracy. Existing training-free compensation methods use an additive bias or a single orthogonal rotation on the output side of the retained weight. These corrections leave its input singular frame unchanged and therefore limit how the retained weight can adapt after column removal. We propose COEC (Calibrated Orthogonal-Equivalence Compensation), a training-free compensation framework that applies alternating left and right orthogonal rotations to the retained weight. The right rotation is optimized on a reduced Stiefel manifold, while singular values are rescaled using generalized cross-validation to select the regularization strength for each layer. COEC further tempers the calibration Gram matrix to reduce the dominance of high-energy activation directions and introduces an alignment penalty that preserves the geometric relation between adjacent attention projections.All components use second-order statistics from a small calibration set and require neither backpropagation through the LLM nor retraining of the model parameters. COEC is independent of the column pruning criterion and can be applied to multiple structured pruning methods. Experiments on the Llama-3, Llama-3.1, and Qwen2.5 model families across multiple structured sparsity levels show that COEC improves perplexity on every model and zero-shot accuracy in most settings over existing compensation methods, with larger gains at higher sparsity. These results show that post-pruning compensation can recover part of the performance lost to column removal.

</details>


### [221] [Capturing Cardiac Cyclicity through Phase-Equivariant Self-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21147)
*Blaise Delaney,Dominic Dootson,Juan Jose Juan Castella,Salil Patel,Andrew Pfaff,Yuji Xing,Jonny Hancox,Karin Sevegnani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Winder，一种利用心动周期相位的等变性作为自监督先验的联合嵌入架构。Winder将表征组织为相位不变坐标与相位旋转谐波子空间，其传输算子是闭式解、无参数。在PTB-XL上以约100万参数规模达到了与SOTA自监督方法相当的诊断精度，同时展现相位等变潜空间几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生理过程的循环结构为自监督表示学习提供了天然先验，而心动周期是其中定义最清晰的场景。作者旨在显式利用心脏周期的相位对称性，以在不增加参数的情况下保留诊断信息并产生可解释的表征。

Method: 1) 推导了一个相位等变的自监督目标函数；2) 提出Winder联合嵌入架构，将表征分解为相位不变坐标和相位旋转的谐波子空间；3) 传输算子是固定闭式解，由循环几何直接推导而非学习得到，不引入额外参数；4) 在PTB-XL上采用冻结线性探针协议进行评测。

Result: Winder在PTB-XL上达到了SOTA自监督方法报告范围内的诊断准确率，参数规模约为100万；同时展现出相位等变的潜空间几何性质。

Conclusion: 显式编码心脏相位对称性能够在保持诊断信息的同时，产生可读性良好、参数高效且与可测量的生理量直接关联的潜空间几何，为生理信号自监督学习提供了新的方向。

Abstract: The cyclic structure of physiological processes offers a natural prior for self-supervised representation learning, and the cardiac cycle provides a particularly well-defined setting in which to exploit it. We derive a phase-equivariant self-supervised objective and introduce Winder, a joint-embedding architecture that organises representations into phase-invariant coordinates and phase-rotating harmonic subspaces. Its transport operator is fixed and closed-form, derived from the cycle's geometry rather than learned, and adds no parameters. Evaluated on PTB-XL under a frozen linear-probe protocol, Winder attains diagnostic accuracy within the range reported by state-of-the-art self-supervised methods at a ~1 M parameter footprint, while exhibiting phase-equivariant latent geometry. These findings demonstrate that explicitly encoding cardiac-phase symmetry can preserve diagnostically useful information while yielding a latent geometry that is legible, parameter-efficient, and directly tied to a measurable physiological quantity.

</details>


### [222] [Thermo-FL: Thermal-Aware Robust Federated Fine-Tuning of Large Language Models for Edge AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21172)
*Shiva Shrestha,Kazi Shaharair Sharif,Zongxing Xie,Jiajing Huang,Anhao Xiang,Honghui Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Thermo-FL是一个热感知的联邦LoRA微调框架，利用设备温度作为控制信号来调节本地适配器训练和稀疏更新传输，并通过TERRA鲁棒聚合管道抵御拜占庭客户端和通信层对手的攻击。在模拟器和Jetson物理测试平台上均验证了其在热稳定性、鲁棒性和模型效用方面的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦微调使大语言模型能够在边缘设备上适配，无需集中私有数据，但实际部署中必须同时应对硬件不稳定性和对抗性更新破坏。热受限客户端可能因降频而变慢或延迟同步聚合，而拜占庭客户端和通信层对手可能破坏用于构建全局模型的更新。现有方法未同时考虑这些问题。

Method: Thermo-FL框架包含两个关键部分：(1) 客户端侧：使用设备温度作为主动控制信号，动态调整活跃LoRA层比例和传输的更新密度，在热应力下降低工作负载；(2) 服务器侧：提出TERRA鲁棒聚合管道，用于动态稀疏LoRA更新，包含范数过滤、掩码感知方向验证、自适应活跃坐标裁剪和掩码感知聚合。

Result: 在大规模模拟器中，Thermo-FL在对抗性稀疏聚合下提升了鲁棒性，在清洁和攻击设置下均取得最佳BoolQ准确率，并在GSM8K上保持竞争力。在Jetson物理测试平台上，Thermo-FL稳定了设备温度，通过位图稀疏编码减少压缩上传大小，并在符号翻转/缩放和中间人攻击扰动下保持了GSM8K效用。

Conclusion: 安全的边缘LLM适配应联合考虑硬件行为、工作负载调节、稀疏通信和聚合鲁棒性，Thermo-FL证明了这种综合方法的有效性。

Abstract: Federated fine-tuning enables large language models to adapt on edge devices without centralizing private data, but practical deployments must address hardware instability and adversarial update corruption together. Thermally constrained clients may throttle, slow local training, or delay synchronous aggregation, while Byzantine clients and communication-layer adversaries can corrupt the updates used to form the global model. To address these challenges, we present Thermo-FL, a thermal-aware federated LoRA fine-tuning framework that uses device temperature as an active control signal for local adapter training and sparse update transmission. On the client side, Thermo-FL adjusts the active LoRA-layer fraction and transmitted update density as devices heat or cool, reducing workload under thermal stress. On the server side, Thermo-FL introduces TERRA, a robust aggregation pipeline for dynamically sparse LoRA updates that combines norm filtering, mask-aware directional validation, adaptive active-coordinate clipping, and mask-aware aggregation. We evaluate Thermo-FL using both a large-scale emulator and a Jetson-based physical testbed. In the emulator, Thermo-FL improves robustness under adversarial sparse aggregation and achieves the strongest BoolQ accuracy across clean and attack settings while remaining competitive on GSM8K. In the physical prototype, Thermo-FL stabilizes device temperature, reduces compressed upload size through bitmap sparse encoding, and preserves GSM8K utility under sign-flip/scale and MITM perturbations. These results show that secure edge LLM adaptation should jointly consider hardware behavior, workload regulation, sparse communication, and aggregation robustness.

</details>


### [223] [A Neurosymbolic Approach for Constructing Planning Domain Models from Clinical Narratives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21186)
*Ranveer Singh,Saurabh Mathur,Michael Skinner,Prasad Tadepalli,Kristian Kersting,Sriraam Natarajan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NSPIN，一个神经符号框架，用于从非结构化临床叙事中归纳概率规划领域模型（PPDDL）。该方法利用预训练LLM提取事件序列，结合LLM提出的前提条件修订和实证验证来构建规划模型，在2660份腹腔镜阑尾切除术笔记上验证了其泛化能力和临床一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外科手术（如腹腔镜阑尾切除术）是复杂且高风险的过程，但缺少结构化事件数据，且临床叙事中存在大量隐式动作，导致传统经验符号方法和LLM都无法单独有效地归纳概率规划领域模型。因此需要一种结合两者优势的神经符号方法来解决这一挑战。

Method: NSPIN框架包含两个核心阶段：(1) 使用预训练LLM从原始文本中提取并填充（impute）结构化事件序列；(2) 基于提取的事件序列归纳PPDDL概率规划模型，并通过LLM提出的前提条件修订进行迭代优化，同时以经验验证作为指导信号。

Result: 在9位外科医生书写的2660份腹腔镜阑尾切除术笔记上进行评估，NSPIN产生的模型能够泛化到未见过的笔记数据，且专家临床审查表明其归纳出的知识大部分与外科手术实践一致。

Conclusion: NSPIN有效解决了从非结构化临床文本中归纳概率规划领域模型的难题，证明了符号方法（规划模型归纳）与神经方法（LLM）结合的有效性，为手术工作流的形式化建模和决策支持提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Surgical procedures such as laparoscopic appendectomy are complex, high-stakes processes, yet formalizing their workflows for decision support remains a significant challenge. Inducing probabilistic planning domain models in this setting is particularly difficult due to the lack of structured event data and the prevalence of implicit actions in clinical narratives, which neither empirical symbolic methods nor Large Language Models (LLMs) can adequately address on their own. We introduce NSPIN, a neurosymbolic framework for inducing probabilistic planning domain models from unstructured clinical narratives. Our method extracts and imputes structured event sequences from raw text using a pretrained LLM, then induces a PPDDL model and refines its preconditions with LLM-proposed revisions, guided by empirical validation. We evaluate the approach on 2,660 laparoscopic appendectomy notes written by 9 surgeons. NSPIN yields models that generalize to unseen notes, and expert clinical review indicates its induced knowledge is largely consistent with surgical practice.

</details>


### [224] [Tydra: An Efficient Hybrid Model for Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21199)
*Mieszko Komisarczyk,Saurabh Mathur,Maurice Kraus,Sriraam Natarajan,Kristian Kersting*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Tydra，一种用于表格数据上下文学习的混合Transformer-状态空间模型（SSM）架构，通过交错排列注意力层和SSM层来平衡预测性能与计算效率。在30个OpenML数据集上，Tydra相较TabPFN减少了30%的推理时间，同时保持了大部分预测性能，并且优于约十倍规模更大的Hydra模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于Transformer的表格基础模型（如TabPFN）预测性能强但计算代价随上下文长度呈二次增长；而基于次二次SSM的替代方案（如Hydra）虽高效却牺牲了准确性。作者希望找到一种既能保持性能又能提升效率的平衡方案。

Method: 提出混合架构Tydra，将注意力层和SSM层交错排列，融合Transformer的强表达能力与SSM的次二次计算复杂度，专为表格数据上下文学习设计。

Result: 在30个OpenML数据集上，Tydra相比TabPFN将推理时间降低约30%，同时保留了大部分预测性能；相比约十倍规模更大的Hydra模型，Tydra在预测性能上更优且推理速度更快。

Conclusion: 混合架构是表格基础模型未来发展的一个很有前景的方向，能够在性能和效率之间实现良好的权衡。

Abstract: Transformer-based tabular foundation models such as TabPFN achieve strong predictive performance but incur quadratic computational cost with context length. On the other hand, subquadratic SSM-based alternatives such as Hydra trade away accuracy for efficiency. To balance both, we introduce Tydra, a hybrid Transformer-State Space Model (SSM) architecture for tabular in-context learning that interleaves attention and SSM layers. Across 30 OpenML datasets, Tydra reduces inference time by 30% relative to TabPFN while retaining much of its predictive performance. Tydra also outperforms an approximately ten-times-larger Hydra model while providing faster inference. The results indicate that hybrid architectures are a promising direction for tabular foundation models.

</details>


### [225] [Curriculum-Aware Interpolate-then-Refine: Learned Physiological Time-Series Imputation under Realistic Missingness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21207)
*Yu-Chao Huang,Haochen Zhang,Nicholas Konz,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Curriculum-Aware Interpolate-then-Refine（CAIR）框架，一种用于生理时间序列插补的两阶段方法，通过粗插值-逐步精炼的架构以及随机缺口课程训练，在连续血糖监测和重症监护动脉血压数据上，于所有缺失机制（MCAR、MAR、NMAR）下均取得最优精度，并证明了低重建误差并不等同于保留临床负担指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生理时间序列（如动脉血压、血糖）的缺失是临床数据中的普遍问题，但现有插补方法在此领域表现不佳——简单的线性插值甚至优于所有基于学习的插补器。原因是生理缺失具有两个被通用插补器忽视的特性：缺失可能发生在信号临床极端（而非典型）的时刻，且缺口长度可能跨数量级变化。

Method: CAIR采用两阶段框架：双向GRU插值器先学习一条粗略基准曲线，再由Transformer精炼器对自身估计进行三次连续修正，逐步逼近生理真实性。整个模型在信号无关的随机缺口课程下联合训练（从简单到困难的缺口模式渐进学习）。评估采用按缺口长度和缺失机制（MCAR、MAR、NMAR）分层的方式，而非单一平均指标。

Result: 在AI-READI连续血糖监测和MIMIC-III重症监护动脉血压两个数据集上，CAIR在每种缺失机制下均为最准确的插补器。相对于最强基线的优势随难度增大而扩大：MCAR下提升9%，值依赖缺失（NMAR）下提升19%。此外，仅匹配CAIR误差水平的插补方法无法保留临床负担指标，而能恢复这些指标的插补器精度远低；CAIR是唯一在精度和临床指标两个维度上均排名靠前的方法。

Conclusion: CAIR证明了两阶段粗插值-逐步精炼的策略，结合信号无关的随机缺口课程训练，能够有效应对生理信号缺失的极端值与跨数量级缺口特性。同时强调，插补评估不能仅看重建误差，临床行动所依赖的负担性指标必须被纳入评估标准。

Abstract: Imputing physiological time series (arterial blood pressure, blood glucose, etc.) is essential for addressing the missingness that pervades clinical data. Yet modern imputation methods perform poorly in this domain: a recent benchmark found that simple linear interpolation outperformed every learned imputer on real-world clinical signals with realistic gaps. We show that this reflects two properties of physiological missingness that generic imputers ignore: gaps may occur when the signal is clinically extreme rather than typical, and gap lengths can easily span orders of magnitude. To this end, we introduce Curriculum-Aware Interpolate-then-Refine (CAIR), a two-stage framework for physiological time-series imputation. Our key motivation is to learn a coarse base curve and then repeatedly correct it toward physiological realism, rather than predict a gap in a single pass. Consequently, CAIR couples a bidirectional-GRU interpolator with a Transformer refiner that corrects its own estimate over three successive passes, trained jointly under a broad, signal-agnostic random-gap curriculum. We evaluate imputers stratified by gap length and missingness mechanism (MCAR, MAR, NMAR) rather than by a single average, and CAIR is the most accurate under every mechanism on continuous glucose monitoring (AI-READI) and arterial pressure in intensive care (MIMIC-III). Its margin over the strongest baseline grows with difficulty, from 9% under MCAR to 19% under value-dependent dropout, where generic learned imputers are weakest. We further show low reconstruction error alone does not recover the burden metrics clinicians act on: interpolants matching CAIR's error fail to preserve those metrics, imputers that recover them are far less accurate, and CAIR alone ranks among the best on both axes.

</details>


### [226] [TRACE-C: Rank-Calibrated Relational Anomaly Detection for Multi-Stream Operational Telemetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21251)
*Matthew Faucher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TRACE-C是一个用于对齐多流遥测数据的可审计、严格先验的秩校准异常检测器，通过Fisher聚合三个窗口通道（局部最大归一化求和、高斯copula形式依赖对比、AR(1)创新分数）的秩来检测流级别在各自范围内但联合异常的运行遥测。在2019-2020年英国电网数据上的评估显示其能排定Storm Atiyah等事件的排名，但披露的通道消融实验揭示其排名主要归因于局部通道而非copula通道，且2020年无窗口被选中的结果被解释为记录规则饱和而非无事件年份。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 操作遥测数据可能每个单独流都处于各自熟悉范围内，但联合来看却是异常的。作者旨在开发一种能够检测这种"联合异常但个体正常"情况的可审计检测器，同时避免事后偏差（post-hoc selection），并解决检测器内部的透明度问题——不仅要给出排名，还要披露每个通道的贡献。

Method: TRACE-C采用严格先验的秩校准方法：同一体制下的滚动中位数/MAD残差被馈入三个窗口通道——最大归一化局部和、基于稳健z残差的高斯copula形式依赖对比、以及最差标准化AR(1)创新分数。三个通道的秩通过Fisher方法聚合，并与此前的聚合值进行排名比较。评估使用2019年1-4月拟合、7-12月开发证据和2020年冻结的hold-out数据。

Result: 在六个英国电网数据流上，TRACE-C将Storm Atiyah排在2019年测试窗口第一位，但通道消融实验显示该排名归因于局部通道而非copula形式通道（copula-only将Atiyah排在第59位）。9月8日的短频率事件被融合检测器排在第143位（远低于时间通道单独的40位），而重建基线将其排第一。2020年没有任何窗口被选中，这与记录规则饱和一致而非无事件年份；最高排名的冻结窗口后来被解读为Storm Ellen。

Conclusion: 论文强调了三个贯穿始终的解释限制：(1) 得到的p值是选择量而非事件概率；(2) copula形式通道并非字面意义上的copula密度——方法没有应用概率积分或正态分数变换；(3) 经验秩计数是诊断工具，而非覆盖率或伪发现率证明。所有表格和图形都从提交的机器可读报告中生成，强调可审计性。

Abstract: Operational telemetry can be jointly anomalous while every individual stream stays inside its familiar range. TRACE-C is an auditable strictly-prior rank-calibrated detector for aligned multi-stream telemetry: same-regime rolling median/MAD residuals feed three window channels -- a maximum normalized local sum, a Gaussian copula-form dependence contrast on robust-z residuals, and a worst standardized AR(1) innovation -- whose channel ranks are Fisher-aggregated and ranked against earlier aggregates.
  We evaluate six Great Britain grid streams with a January-April 2019 fit, July-December 2019 development evidence, and a 2020 hold-out frozen before inspection. TRACE-C ranks Storm Atiyah first among 2019 test windows, but a disclosed channel ablation attributes that rank to the local channel, not the copula-form channel: copula-only ranks Atiyah 59th. The short 9 August frequency event is ranked far lower by the fused detector (143) than by the temporal channel alone (40), and reconstruction baselines rank it first. In 2020 no window is selected, which is consistent with record-rule saturation rather than an uneventful year; the highest-ranked frozen window was later interpreted as Storm Ellen.
  Three interpretive limits carry throughout. The resulting p-values are selection quantities, not event probabilities. The copula-form channel is not a literal copula density: the method applies no probability-integral or normal-score transform. Empirical rank counts are diagnostics, not coverage or false-discovery proofs. Every table and figure in this paper is generated from committed machine-readable reports.

</details>


### [227] [ConceptTS: LLM-Guided Concept Bottlenecks for Interpretable Multivariate Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21277)
*Yichen Jiang,Yueqiao Chen,Dongyu Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ConceptTS 是一个可解释的多变量时间序列预测框架，利用大语言模型自动生成命名概念及标签规则，通过三个互补的瓶颈结构（历史上下文、局部预测区间、完整预测视界）组织概念，在保持与黑盒基线相当预测精度的同时提供语义可解释的概念激活和直接的概念级干预能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的先进多变量时间序列预测模型虽然能建模复杂的时序依赖和跨变量依赖，但其表示是黑盒的，无法解释特定预测结果产生的原因。这种缺乏透明性的问题限制了模型在需要理解并评估预测背后因素的实践场景中的应用。

Method: ConceptTS 框架利用大语言模型提出任务相关的命名概念并生成可执行的标签规则，将语言模型的领域知识转化为直接监督信号，无需昂贵的人工概念标注。提出的概念被组织为三个互补的瓶颈结构，分别描述历史上下文、局部预测区间和完整预测视界。一个共享解码器结合从预期激活中学习到的表示来构建最终预测，使决策过程显式化并支持概念层面的直接干预。

Result: 在北京多站点空气质量数据集上的实验表明，ConceptTS 在预测精度上与强黑盒基线模型具有竞争力，同时能够产生语义上有意义的概念激活。

Conclusion: ConceptTS 证明了通过大语言模型自动生成概念和标签规则的方式，可以在不牺牲预测性能的前提下实现可解释的时间序列预测，为可解释人工智能在时间序列领域的应用提供了可行路径。

Abstract: State-of-the-art multivariate time-series forecasters can model complex temporal and cross-variable dependencies, yet their opaque representations provide limited insight into why a particular forecast is produced. This lack of transparency restricts their use in settings where practitioners must understand and assess the factors underlying a prediction. We introduce ConceptTS, an interpretable forecasting framework that organizes its predictions around named, human-readable concepts. ConceptTS uses a large language model to propose task-relevant concepts and generate executable labeling rules, translating the language model's domain knowledge into direct supervision without costly manual concept annotation. The proposed concepts are organized into three complementary bottlenecks that describe the historical context, local forecast intervals, and the full forecast horizon. A shared decoder combines representations derived from their predicted activations to construct the forecast, making the model's decision process explicit and supporting direct concept-level interventions. Experiments on the Beijing Multi-Site Air Quality dataset show that ConceptTS achieves accuracy competitive with strong black-box baselines while producing semantically meaningful concept activations.

</details>


### [228] [SPARCL: Spectral Partitioned Analytic Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21307)
*James Hartley,Zeropy Surio,Daniel Whitmore,Hannah Clarke,Thomas Reed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SPARCL，一种基于谱分解的解析持续学习方法，通过将自相关矩阵分解为高能核心与残差互补两部分，冻结旧类分类器在核心子空间中的分量，仅通过递归最小二乘更新残差块，从而缓解解析持续学习中因谱干扰导致的旧类漂移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管解析持续学习通过闭式岭回归取代了梯度迭代优化，但旧类仍会出现漂移。传统基于随机梯度覆盖的遗忘叙事无法解释这一现象。作者识别出根本原因在于谱干扰：所有任务共享同一个逆自相关算子，新任务样本在旧主导特征方向上的载荷会稀释频谱并扰动旧类logits。

Method: 提出SPARCL（Spectral PARTitioned Analytic Continual Learner）：将运行中的自相关矩阵分解为高能核心子空间和残差互补子空间；冻结核心子空间中旧类分类器分量；仅通过递归最小二乘更新残差块，并可选地引入残差随机投影扩展。该更新为闭式形式，并具有核心贡献不变性的可证明保证。

Result: 在CIFAR-100、CUB-200、ImageNet-R和ImageNet-A上，采用冻结ViT-B/16协议，SPARCL显著缩小了经典解析学习器与强表示匹配方法之间的性能差距，且与稀疏特征去相关方法（如Fly-CL）具有互补性。

Conclusion: 谱干扰是解析持续学习中旧类漂移的关键机制，通过谱分区冻结核心子空间可以有效地在解析框架内实现旧类保护，SPARCL为解析持续学习提供了一种简单闭式且具有理论保证的改进方案。

Abstract: Analytic continual learning has emerged as a strong exemplar-free alternative to gradient-based class-incremental learning because it replaces iterative optimization with closed-form ridge updates. Yet the usual forgetting narrative, centered on stochastic gradient overwriting, does not explain why analytic methods still drift on old classes despite exact recursive solvers. We identify the culprit as spectral interference: the joint ridge classifier for all tasks shares the inverse autocorrelation operator $(R+λI)^{-1}$, so incoming task samples that load onto old dominant eigendirections dilute the spectrum and perturb old-class logits even when old labels are never revisited. Based on this view, we propose SPARCL, a spectral partitioned analytic continual learner that decomposes the running autocorrelation into a high-energy core and a residual complement, freezes old-class classifier components in the core subspace, and updates only the residual block through recursive least squares with an optional residual random-projection expansion. This yields a simple closed-form update with a provable invariance guarantee for the core contribution of old logits. Across CIFAR-100, CUB-200, ImageNet-R, and ImageNet-A under a frozen ViT-B/16 protocol, SPARCL closes most of the gap from classical analytic learners to strong representation matchers, while remaining complementary to sparse feature-decorrelation approaches such as Fly-CL.

</details>


### [229] [Rethinking Expressivity and Efficiency in Test-Time Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21308)
*Zeyun Zhong,Joya Chen,Manuel Martin,Frederik Diederichs,Juergen Gall,Juergen Beyerer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: E²-TTT提出了一种兼具表达力与硬件效率的测试时训练（TTT）方法，通过推导封闭形式的块级状态转移精确复现逐token循环的快速权重与动量状态，实现了完全并行的块级训练，在1.3B参数模型上达到与现有TTT和混合注意力基线相当的语言建模性能，同时在上下文检索和长度外推（8倍训练长度下保留90%以上准确率）上表现更优，并匹配高效块级方法的训练吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TTT方法在处理长上下文时面临两难困境：逐token更新动态具有高表达力但硬件效率低，而块级近似方法虽然高效却牺牲了更新规则的时序结构。

Method: 在块起始权重处取梯度的标准近似下，推导出精确复现逐token循环在块末尾快权重和动量状态的封闭形式状态转移公式，从而实现完全并行化的块级训练，同时保留此前块级方法舍弃的更新规则时序结构。

Result: 从头训练了高达1.3B参数的模型，在语言建模上与现有TTT和混合注意力基线持平，在上下文检索上超越它们；在"大海捞针"测试中，在8倍训练上下文长度下仍保持90%以上准确率；训练吞吐量与高效块级方法匹配。

Conclusion: E²-TTT有效调和了表达力与效率之间的矛盾，证明了可以在保持逐token更新表达力的同时实现块级并行训练效率，代码已开源。

Abstract: Test-Time Training (TTT) enables long-context processing via continuous weight updates during inference, but current methods struggle to balance the expressivity of per-token update dynamics with the hardware efficiency of chunk-wise approximations. We propose E$^2$-TTT (Expressive and Efficient TTT) to bridge this gap. Under the standard approximation of taking gradients at the chunk-start weights, we derive a closed-form state transition that exactly reproduces the chunk-end fast-weight and momentum states of the per-token recurrence. This enables fully parallelized chunk-level training while preserving the temporal structure of the update rule that prior chunk-wise methods discard. We validate E$^2$-TTT by training models up to 1.3B parameters from scratch. It performs on par with previous TTT and hybrid attention baselines in language modeling while outperforming them on in-context retrieval. Its advantage is most pronounced in length extrapolation: on the standard ``Needle in a Haystack'' passkey test, it retains over 90% accuracy at $8\times$ the training context length. Meanwhile, E$^2$-TTT can match the training throughput of efficient chunk-wise methods, demonstrating that it effectively reconciles expressivity with efficiency. The code is available at https://github.com/zeyun-zhong/E2-TTT.

</details>


### [230] [Time-Aware Tranformer-Based Prediction Model for AECOPD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21324)
*Weihao Qu,Ling Zheng,Dongyang Wang,Jiacun Wang,Haowen Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于时间感知Transformer的AECOPD（慢性阻塞性肺疾病急性加重）预测模型，利用家用呼吸机的日常呼吸数据实现及时检测，避免了传统临床检验数据带来的延迟，在多项分类任务中优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AECOPD症状变化迅速，需要时间敏感的预测模型。然而现有机器学习模型依赖临床和实验室数据，不可避免地造成检测延迟，无法满足家庭监测场景下及时检测的需求。

Method: 提出Time-Aware transformer-based AECOPD预测模型，使用时间感知Transformer从日常家用呼吸机的呼吸数据中捕获症状及其时间进展信息，生成有意义的患者表征。

Result: 实验结果表明，该时间感知Transformer方法在多项分类任务中均优于传统方法，展示了提升AECOPD预测准确率的潜力。

Conclusion: 时间感知Transformer在家用呼吸机数据上有效预测AECOPD，能够实现低延迟检测，有望增强AECOPD预测准确性并改善临床结局。

Abstract: The rapid symptom change of Acute exacerbation of chronic obstructive pulmonary disease (AECOPD) makes it critical to have time-sensitive prediction models. However, most current machine learning models studying AECOPD use clinical and laboratory data, which will inevitably cause latency. To ensure timely detection of AECOPD and minimize latency, this paper focuses on home monitoring scenarios where only respiratory data from daily-use ventilators is available. We introduce a Time-Aware transformer-based AECOPD prediction model, which generates meaningful patient representations using the Time-Aware transformer to capture the symptoms and their temporal progression in ventilator data. Our experimental results demonstrate that our Time-Aware transformer-based approach outperforms traditional methods in multiple classification tasks, highlighting its potential to enhance AECOPD prediction accuracy.

</details>


### [231] [Across-Design Uncertainty in Short Pricing Panels: Evidence from Simulated Price Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21334)
*Pedro Cadahia Delgado*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究稀疏定价面板数据中估计误差的推理性后果，将"给定价格轨迹条件下的不确定性"与"跨不同价格轨迹的估计误差变动"分离。在基线模拟中，后者占梯度提升规格估计误差方差的97.6%，表明传统面板内重抽样无法捕捉跨设计间差异。研究提出经验关系σ̂≈0.182·V^(-0.271)来描述跨设计离散度，并发现Paule-Mandel方差成分估计可将经验覆盖率从0.469提升至0.931。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏定价面板数据在观测数量上庞大但价格变动次数极少，这种特征导致传统推理方法（如面板内重抽样）无法反映数据生成过程本身所固有的跨轨迹变异性。作者旨在识别并量化这一问题，为定价研究中的统计推断提供更准确的框架。

Method: 采用校准至稀疏定价机制的合成数据生成过程，将不确定性分解为给定已实现价格轨迹条件下的条件不确定性和跨替代轨迹的估计误差变化。通过基线模拟量化各成分贡献，使用经验关系拟合跨设计离散度，并采用Paule-Mandel方差成分法估计跨独立定价单元的方差，检验其在统计推断中的覆盖率表现。

Result: 研究发现跨设计成分占梯度提升规格估计误差方差的97.6%；跨设计离散度由关系σ̂≈0.182·V^(-0.271)很好地描述；共享价格路径的区域增加不能产生独立轨迹，而独立单元平均以平方根速率降低离散度；Paule-Mandel方法将同质模拟中的经验覆盖率从0.469提升至0.931。

Conclusion: 核心启示是应当转向设计能够产生独立识别变异的数4据生成过程，而非仅依赖固定的被动面板。只有创建跨设计层面的独立信息，才能对定价效应进行可靠的统计推断。

Abstract: Short observational pricing panels can contain many observations while offering only a small number of distinct price movements. This paper studies the inferential consequences of that distinction in a synthetic data-generating process calibrated to a sparse pricing regime. We separate uncertainty conditional on a realised price trajectory from variation in estimation error across alternative trajectories generated by the same pricing process. In the baseline simulations, the latter component accounts for 97.6% of the variance of estimation error for the gradient-boosted specification. Within-panel resampling procedures use the information of one realised trajectory and do not identify this across-design component.
  Three results organise the analysis. First, across-design dispersion is well described by the empirical relation sigma_hat approx 0.182 V^(-0.271), where V equals moves times magnitude squared. Second, adding regions sharing a common price path reduces outcome noise but does not create independent price trajectories; conversely, averaging across units with independent design-specific errors reduces dispersion at the standard square root rate. Third, a Paule-Mandel variance component estimated across independently priced units substantially increases empirical coverage in homogeneous simulations, from 0.469 to 0.931. The broader implication is a shift toward designing data-generating processes that create independent identifying variation rather than relying solely on fixed passive panels.

</details>


### [232] [Asymmetric Capacity Allocation in Self-Refinement Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21345)
*Zhuoyi Yang,Ian G. Harris,Salar Hashemitaheri,Cassie Huang,Yuangang Li,Hyunwoo Oh,Paul Dourish,Tony Givargis,Mohsen Imani,Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地研究了自我精炼（self-refinement）流程中各阶段（生成、批判、修订）对模型大小的不同敏感性。通过在5个不同领域的基准测试上，使用Qwen3的6种模型大小和Gemma 3的4种模型大小进行实验，发现生成器和修订器越大越好，而批判器的大小对性能几乎无影响，但即使是小型批判器也比不进行批判效果好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我精炼（生成-批判-修订）是提升LLM生成质量的主流范式，也是许多LLM智能体的核心机制。然而，三个阶段的认知需求不同，现有方法大多将模型大小视为实现细节而非研究对象，可能导致资源浪费。缺乏对模型大小如何影响各阶段的系统性研究。

Method: 首次对不同模型大小下的自我精炼流程进行逐阶段（stage-wise）研究。在5个不同领域的基准测试上，使用Qwen3的6种模型规模和Gemma 3的4种模型规模进行系统性实验，分别考察生成器、批判器和修订器大小变化对整体流水线性能的影响。

Result: 1）更大的生成器和修订器通常能提升流水线整体性能，但过小的修订器甚至可能损害性能；2）性能对批判器大小高度不敏感，但即使加入小型批判器也一致优于完全省略批判环节。

Conclusion: 模型容量不应在自我精炼流水线的各阶段均匀分配。不同阶段展现出不同的规模缩放特性，这为设计更计算高效的多阶段语言模型系统提供了实际指导。

Abstract: Self-refinement, typically structured as generation, critique, and revision, is a widely adopted paradigm for improving LLM generation and serves as a core mechanism in many LLM agents. While the three stages involve different cognitive demands, most existing approaches conveniently treat the model size as an implementation detail rather than a subject of study, which may lead to a waste of resources. Little work has systematically examined how model size affects each stage or whether effective self-refinement requires equally capable models for generation, critique, and revision. We present the first stage-wise model size study of the self-refinement pipeline on 5 benchmarks from different domains using 6 model sizes of Qwen3 and 4 model sizes of Gemma 3. We conclude that larger generators and refiners generally improve the pipeline, whereas an undersized refiner can even harm performance. Second, performance is highly insensitive to the size of the critic, although including even a small critic consistently outperforms omitting critique altogether. Our findings demonstrate that model capacity should not be allocated uniformly across self-refinement pipelines. Instead, different stages exhibit distinct size scaling characteristics, providing practical guidance for designing more computationally efficient multi-stage language model systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.GR'></div>

# cs.GR [[Back]](#toc)

### [233] [Maximum Entropy Encoding of Energy-Weighted Spherical Moments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20429)
*Jiaze Sun*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文研究如何利用有限矩压缩和重建由非负蒙特卡洛路径样本组成的角能量信号（用于辐照度）。采用总能量、一阶方向矩和无迹二阶矩作为1+3+5个线性可加、旋转协变的统计量，在固定Lebesgue参考测度下通过最大熵闭包得到p(r,u)∝exp(-βr g(u))的分布形式。实验表明五参数MaxEnt在981个Poly Haven HDRI场景中相对QZH有78.7%的胜率，平均亮度RMSE降低15.8%，且所有变体均保持零负辐照度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 角能量信号压缩重建是计算机图形学中辐照度计算的关键问题。已有方法如球形谐波（SH）在多次散射场景中可能产生负辐照度，而实际光照环境往往具有强方向性，需要一种既能紧凑压缩又能保证非负重建的方法。

Method: 将每个路径样本表示为能量加权的方向特征x=ru，采用总能量、一阶方向矩和二阶无迹矩（共9个统计量）作为线性可加、旋转协变的统计表示。在固定Lebesgue参考测度下推导最大熵闭包，得到分布形式p(r,u)∝exp(-βr g(u))，其中g(u)=1-b·u+u^T Q u。对纯偶极四参数子族提供解析矩匹配、方差、逆采样和闭式漫反射响应；对偶极-二阶矩共轴五参数子族提供可实现域、配分函数、方位角代数积分和LUT导向的重建形式。

Result: 在981个Poly Haven HDRI 2K场景和三个Debevec探针上实验：五参数MaxEnt相对存储QZH有78.7%每场景胜率，平均亮度RMSE降低15.8%，方向性强的场景优势更明显；两种MaxEnt变体在所有场景中均保持零负辐照度。全二阶SH-2整体误差最低，五参数MaxEnt排名第二且在强方向性场景中超越SH-2；共轴子族在非共轴多源场景中存在系统性闭包误差。

Conclusion: 最大熵闭包为角能量信号压缩提供了一种保证非负重建的紧凑表示框架。五参数MaxEnt在精度与压缩率之间取得了良好的平衡，尤其在强方向性光照场景中优于QZH和SH-2。研究为后续在辐射度量、多次散射等需要非负约束的场景中应用最大熵方法奠定了基础。

Abstract: We study how angular energy signals composed of non-negative Monte Carlo path samples can be compressed and reconstructed for irradiance using finite moments. Writing each sample as an energy-weighted directional feature $x = r u$, we adopt total energy, the first directional moment, and the traceless second moment as $1+3+5$ linearly additive, rotationally covariant statistics. Under a fixed Lebesgue reference measure, the maximum-entropy closure yields $p(r,u) \propto \exp(-βr g(u))$, where $g(u) = 1 - b \cdot u + u^T Q u$, whose directional probability and angular energy density are proportional to $g^{-3}$ and $g^{-4}$, respectively. When $g_{\min} > 0$ the closure is normalizable and the reconstruction is strictly positive. We further provide analytic moment matching, variance, inverse sampling, and closed-form diffuse response for the pure-dipole four-parameter subfamily, as well as the realizability domain, partition function, azimuthal algebraic integral, and LUT-oriented reconstruction form for the dipole-second-moment coaxial five-parameter subfamily. Experiments cover 981 Poly Haven HDRI 2K scenes and three Debevec probes. Five-parameter MaxEnt achieves a 78.7% per-scene win rate against stored QZH, with mean luminance RMSE reduced by 15.8%; the advantage is more pronounced in scenes with strong directionality. Both MaxEnt variants maintain zero negative irradiance across all scenes. Full second-order SH-2 yields the lowest overall error, while five-parameter MaxEnt ranks second and outperforms SH-2 in the high-directionality bucket; the coaxial subfamily shows systematic closure error on non-coaxial multi-source scenes.

</details>


### [234] [MultiCube: Compositional 3D Generation With Part-Level Semantic and Spatial Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20448)
*Ava Pun,Kangle Deng,Yiheng Zhu,Jun-Yan Zhu,Maneesh Agrawala,Tinghui Zhou*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: MultiCube是一种全新的组合式3D生成方法，通过两阶段扩散过程与Part Layout Adapter，实现对3D物体各个部件在语义和空间布局上的独立精确控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的3D生成方法虽然能基于图像或文本提示生成高质量的组合式3D物体，但全局条件化缺乏精细到部件级别的控制能力，无法满足专业创意工作流的需求。

Method: MultiCube以全局文本提示、指定所需部件的文本模式（schema）以及指示各部件包围盒的空间布局为输入，采用两阶段扩散过程：首先生成与模式及布局对齐的整体网格（monolithic mesh），然后同时将网格分解为各个独立部件。其中提出了一种Part Layout Adapter，用于独立于其他部件编码每个部件的条件信息。

Result: 实验表明该方法能够生成高质量的组合式3D物体，具备精确的部件级别控制能力，包括生成那些难以仅通过文本或图像提示实现的独特布局。

Conclusion: MultiCube显著提升了3D生成中部件级语义与空间布局的可控性，为专业创作提供了更灵活精确的工具，扩展了3D生成的应用边界。

Abstract: Digital 3D objects used in games and animation are often required to be compositional; that is, decomposed into semantically meaningful parts. Recent 3D generation methods can produce high-quality compositional objects conditioned on image or text prompts. Yet, such global conditioning lacks the precise part-level controllability required for professional creative workflows. To address this, we introduce MultiCube, a novel compositional 3D generation method that provides explicit, independent control over both the semantics and spatial arrangement of each part. MultiCube takes as input a global text prompt, a text schema specifying the desired parts, and a spatial layout indicating the bounding boxes of the parts in the given schema. It outputs a 3D object composed of distinct meshes, one per specified part, that adhere to the given semantic and spatial conditions. Our approach employs a two-stage diffusion process, first generating a schema- and layout-aligned monolithic mesh, then decomposing the mesh into individual parts simultaneously. A novel Part Layout Adapter is used to encode per-part conditions independently of the other parts. Experiments demonstrate that our method can generate high-quality compositional 3D objects with precise part-level control, including those with unique layouts difficult to achieve with text or image prompting alone. Project page: https://multi-cube.github.io

</details>


### [235] [CubicSplat: Differentiable Vector Graphics via Error-Bounded Forward Relaxation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20803)
*Chenglong Liu,Xin Zhang,Yimeng Zhu,Liyang He,Yixiao Ma,Yu Su,Zhenya Huang,Qi Liu*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: CubicSplat是一种可微矢量光栅化器，用均匀折线替代Bézier最近点求解器，使几何误差有界为O(S^-2)，从而构建静态计算图并产生条件良好的梯度，同时通过合成导出的可见性机制剪枝退化图元。在DIV2K和Kodak基准上达到SOTA重建质量，闭填充设置下PSNR提升超过2dB，训练速度比先前方法快4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矢量图形因分辨率无关、存储紧凑且可直接编辑而受到青睐，对其参数化图元进行可微优化很有吸引力。然而经典光栅化在几何上不连续，现有通过平滑前向传播的解决方案在场景复杂度增加时需要日益繁琐的启发式方法。论文指出根本问题在于梯度跷跷板效应：改善前向几何精确性的设计选择会系统性削弱梯度信号，反之亦然。

Method: CubicSplat用均匀折线代理替代Bézier最近点求解器，其几何误差以O(S^{-2})为界；由此产生的静态计算图从结构上保证梯度条件良好；同时，利用合成导出的可见性机制无需辅助正则化即可剪枝退化图元。

Result: 在DIV2K和Kodak基准上，CubicSplat在闭填充设置中实现SOTA重建质量，PSNR增益超过2dB，且训练速度比先前方法快最多4倍。

Conclusion: 通过折线代理与静态计算图设计，CubicSplat有效化解了可微矢量光栅化中几何精确性与梯度信号之间的跷跷板矛盾，在重建质量和训练效率上均取得显著提升，并已开源代码。

Abstract: Vector graphics are prized for their resolution independence, compact storage, and direct editability, making differentiable optimization of their parametric primitives an attractive goal. Yet classical rasterization is discontinuous with respect to geometry, and existing remedies that smooth the forward pass demand increasingly elaborate heuristics as scene complexity grows. We trace this fragility to a gradient seesaw: design choices that improve forward geometric exactness can systematically degrade the induced gradient signal, and vice versa. To navigate this tension we introduce CubicSplat, a differentiable vector rasterizer that replaces Bézier closest-point solvers with uniform polyline surrogates whose geometric error is bounded at $O(S^{-2})$. The resulting static computation graph yields well-conditioned gradients by construction, while a compositing-derived visibility mechanism prunes degenerate primitives without auxiliary regularization. On DIV2K and Kodak benchmarks CubicSplat achieves state-of-the-art reconstruction quality with over 2 dB PSNR gain in the closed-fill setting, while training up to 4x faster than prior methods. The code is available at https://github.com/CubicSplat/repo

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [236] [SDAD: Spec-Driven Agentic Development for the AI-Native SDLC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20341)
*Vu Hung Nguyen,Thanh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"规范驱动的智能体开发"（SDAD）范式，将严格的规格形式化与高速度的智能体实施相融合，主张智能体速度不会消除工程纪律，而是将纪律前移至规范精确性、显式门禁和可审计溯源之中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型驱动的编码智能体凭借超大上下文窗口正重塑软件开发生命周期，使得规范质量成为自主交付的执行燃料。作者旨在形式化一种新范式，回应Waterfall与Agile之间的历史摇摆，并解决智能体时代下工程纪律如何维持、角色如何演变以及治理如何量化的问题。

Method: 以SDAD为核心框架，涵盖四个环节：意图捕获、机器可读规范、智能体综合、与人类签署下的独立多智能体验证。横向对比Human-Agile（约2020）与Agentic-SDAD（约2026）在产品、节奏、问责和安全态势方面的差异；纵向扩展至团队角色转型、量化治理指标（歧义税、规范保真度、SER、带修复乘数phi的TCI_agentic）以及分阶段迁移蓝图与混合估算的采用路径。

Result: 论文将AI代码确立为第四大生产范式，整合了AI增强测试与验证的工业及研究证据，以论证综合权与发布权分离的必要性，并给出了量化治理模型（含歧义税、规范保真度等指标）及务实采用指南。

Conclusion: 智能体驱动的开发速度并不会替代工程纪律，而是把纪律从实施阶段重新定位到规范制定、显式验收门禁与可审计溯源等上游环节，从而实现纪律与速度的辩证统一。

Abstract: Frontier coding agents backed by large language models with context windows from hundreds of thousands to millions of tokens are restructuring the Software Development Life Cycle (SDLC). Rich context handling and multi-step reasoning now allow substantial Functional Requirement Documents (FRDs) and repository context to be ingested in a single workflow, making specification quality the execution fuel for autonomous delivery. This report formalises Spec-Driven Agentic Development (SDAD) as a synthesis of disciplined up-front formalisation and high-velocity implementation: intent capture, machine-readable specification, agentic synthesis, and independent multi-agent verification under human sign-off. We revisit the historical pendulum between Waterfall and Agile, introduce AI-code as a fourth production paradigm, and compare Human-Agile (circa 2020) with Agentic-SDAD (circa 2026) across artefacts, cadence, accountability, and security posture. Beyond process description, we extend the model to team role metamorphosis (engineer, QA, platform, and product functions), quantitative governance (Ambiguity Tax, Spec Fidelity, SER, and TCI_agentic with repair multiplier phi), and pragmatic adoption via hybrid estimation and a staged migration blueprint. Industrial and research evidence on AI-augmented testing and verification is integrated to motivate separation between synthesis and release authority. Overall, the paper argues that agentic speed does not eliminate engineering discipline; it relocates discipline upstream into specification precision, explicit gates, and auditable provenance.

</details>


### [237] [PrimeAgentOrchestrator: Memory-Primed Agent Spawning for Personal AI Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20342)
*Myron Koch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了PrimeAgentOrchestrator（PAO）系统，它为LLM编码代理（Claude Code）在启动时预加载从用户个人数据库中编译的上下文记忆，通过并行查询两个独立的记忆后端（PostgreSQL实体-观察数据库和Cloudflare Worker语义搜索索引）并融合结果，以文件系统注入方式传递给代理。文章报告了2025年12月至2026年3月四个月的部署经验，记录了三种上下文传递机制的迭代及各自的失败模式和工程权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM编码代理每次会话都从空上下文窗口开始，无法继承之前工作的累积知识，导致代理缺乏跨会话的记忆连续性，浪费了先前工作中积累的有价值信息。

Method: PAO系统在代理生成时并行查询两个独立的记忆后端：一个PostgreSQL实体-观察数据库和一个Cloudflare Worker语义搜索索引，使用后端特定的检索策略融合结果，并通过文件系统注入（利用宿主代理的配置自动读取行为）将编译后的上下文简报传递给Claude Code。系统管理完整的代理生命周期，包括信任预置、带错误检测的就绪轮询和自适应终端文本注入。

Result: 系统经过四个月（2025年12月至2026年3月）的常规部署，展示了三代上下文传递机制的演进，记录了每种机制重新设计背后的失败模式，以及桥接异构记忆系统而非构建统一系统的工程权衡。

Conclusion: PAO通过桥接异构记忆系统为LLM编码代理提供跨会话的上下文记忆，该经验报告揭示了在实践中将多个异构记忆后端集成到代理生命周期中的具体挑战、迭代过程与工程取舍。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) coding agents start each session with an empty context window, discarding accumulated knowledge from prior work. We present PrimeAgentOrchestrator (PAO), a system that spawns new instances of Claude Code -- Anthropic's terminal-based coding agent -- pre-loaded with relevant memories compiled from the user's existing personal databases. At spawn time, PAO queries two independently-operated memory backends in parallel (a PostgreSQL entity-observation database and a Cloudflare Worker semantic search index), fuses results using backend-specific retrieval strategies, and delivers the compiled briefing via filesystem injection that exploits the host agent's configuration auto-read behavior. PAO manages the full agent lifecycle including trust pre-seeding, readiness polling with error detection, and adaptive terminal text injection. We report on four months of regular deployment (December 2025 through March 2026) as an experience report, documenting three generations of context delivery mechanisms, the failure modes that motivated each redesign, and the engineering tradeoffs of bridging heterogeneous memory systems rather than building a unified one.

</details>


### [238] [Truth Lies Deep: Countering Semantic Camouflage via Latent Intent Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20378)
*Md. Hasib Ur Rahman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究揭示了大语言模型安全对齐的表面性问题，提出"语义伪装"攻击可绕过现有防护机制，并基于对潜在激活轨迹的分析，提出轻量级探测防御方法LIV，在多个模型架构上显著提升对零日语义攻击的检测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM的安全对齐往往停留在表面，仅依赖生成末端的拒绝机制，而未消除预训练阶段获得的有害概念知识。这种架构性脱节使得模型容易受到将恶意意图包装在良性叙事语境（如创意写作）中的对抗性攻击，绕过标准输入输出防护栏。

Method: 首先通过分析三种SLM系列（Phi-3、Qwen2.5、Gemma-2b）在对抗压力下的潜在激活轨迹，识别出通用的"意图地平线"（Intent Horizon）——通常在总层数的15-20%深度处，模型对有害意图的独特预训练表示发生坍缩并转为"安全"叙事的上下文表征。基于早期层保留的独特"有害特征"，提出潜在意图验证（LIV）轻量级探测防御方法。

Result: 晚期层的伪装攻击表示与安全查询在数学上不可区分（检测率低于20%），但早期层保留可检测的"有害特征"。在PKU-SafeRLHF数据集上，LIV在所有测试架构上比标准防护栏性能提升20-50%，无需重新训练即可有效中和零日语义攻击。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了语义伪装攻击的有效性源于安全对齐的浅层性，并验证了利用早期层表征进行轻量级防御的可行性。LIV提供了一种无需模型重训练的高效检测方案，为LLM安全防护开辟了新思路。

Abstract: Safety alignment in Large Language Models (LLMs) is often superficial, relying on refusal mechanisms that trigger only at the final stages of generation without erasing the foundational knowledge of harmful concepts acquired during pretraining. This study demonstrates that this architectural disconnect leaves models vulnerable to Semantic Camouflage -- adversarial attacks that wrap harmful intent in benign narrative contexts (e.g., creative writing), effectively bypassing standard input and output guardrails. By analyzing the latent activation trajectories of three distinct Small Language Model (SLM) families (Phi-3, Qwen2.5, and Gemma-2b) under adversarial stress, this research identifies a universal ``Intent Horizon'' -- a critical depth (typically 15--20\% of total layers) where the model's distinct, pre-trained representation of harmful intent collapses as it contextualizes the query into a ``safe'' narrative. Results indicate that while late-layer representations of camouflaged attacks are mathematically indistinguishable from safe queries (Detection Rate $< 20\%$), early-layer representations retain a distinct, detectable ``harm signature.'' Leveraging this insight, this paper proposes Latent Intent Verification (LIV), a lightweight probing defense. Experiments on the PKU-SafeRLHF dataset demonstrate that LIV outperforms standard guardrails by a margin of 20--50\% across all tested architectures, effectively neutralizing zero-day semantic attacks without requiring model retraining.

</details>


### [239] [A Survey on Foundations and Frontiers of Multimodal Agentic Frameworks: Techniques and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20379)
*Neel Mokaria,Rishie Raj,Dheeraj Baiju,Xiaoqian Shen,Shraman Pramanick,Kevin Qinghong Lin,Arda Senocak,Mike Zheng Shou,Philip Torr,Mohamed Elhoseiny,Yapeng Tian,Ruohan Gao,Salman Khan,Sayan Nag,Sanjoy Chowdhury,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统性地综述了多模态在智能体（Agent）框架中的作用，围绕感知、推理、规划、记忆和行动五大核心功能模块，分析多模态如何赋能智能体从文本中心演化到多模态框架，并构建了以模态为中心的分类体系，涵盖多个应用场景及效率-扩展性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有大量关于LLM智能体的综述，但多模态对智能体能力塑造的作用在近年未被系统性地研究。随着大语言模型向大多模态模型演进，智能体系统能够处理图像、音频、视频等多模态信息，亟需填补这一综述空白。

Method: 以智能体框架的核心功能模块（感知、推理、规划、记忆、行动）为分析透镜，追踪从文本中心智能体到多模态框架的演化；通过委托式（delegated）、晚期融合（late-fusion）和早期融合（early-fusion）三种架构分析模态集成方式；提出以模态为中心的分类体系，将架构设计选择与智能体能力相关联；并在多个应用领域（机器人、GUI与网页导航、多媒体内容生成与编辑、长视频理解与检索）进行审查与性能分析，同时讨论效率-扩展性权衡。

Result: 该综述揭示了多模态在智能体各功能模块中的关键作用，总结了从文本中心到多模态框架的演进路径，识别了不同模态集成架构（委托式、晚期融合、早期融合）对智能体行为的影响，并在多个应用领域评估了性能表现及训练/推理成本、延迟和部署约束等效率权衡。

Conclusion: 通过聚焦多模态对智能体设计的影响，本文识别了当前研究中的关键缺口，并为构建稳健、通用的智能系统绘制了未来研究路线图。

Abstract: Advances in large language models (LLMs) have fueled a wave of research into agency: the ability to reason, plan, and act. This effort has produced agentic frameworks that orchestrate perception, memory, and decision-making around powerful LLM backbones. With the advent of large multimodal models (LMMs), these systems can process and integrate diverse modalities, including images, audio, and video, thereby improving their real-world applicability. Yet, while surveys of LLM-based agents exist, the role of multimodality in shaping agency has not been systematically examined in recent years. This survey fills the gap by analyzing the impact of multimodality across the core functional modules of the agentic framework: perception, reasoning, planning, memory, and action. Using this lens, we trace the evolution from text-centric agents to multimodal frameworks, examine how modalities are integrated through delegated, late-fusion, and early-fusion architectures, and assess the emergence of agentic behaviors enabled by grounded perception and multimodal reasoning. We organize existing work through a modality-centric taxonomy that links architectural design choices to agent capabilities. Moreover, we review multimodal agentic systems across various application domains, including Robotics, GUI & Web Navigation, Multimedia Content Generation & Editing, and Long-form Video Understanding & Retrieval. Beyond capabilities, we analyze performance across these settings and discuss efficiency-scalability trade-offs, including training and inference costs, latency, and deployment constraints. By focusing on the impact of multimodality in agentic design, we aim to identify key gaps and chart a roadmap toward robust and general-purpose intelligent systems.

</details>


### [240] [Interpretable Multimodal Classification with Linear Discriminant Tree Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20384)
*Mojtaba Moattari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于树集成（LDT、LDF、LDAB）的多模态情感与行为分类框架，将各模态编码为token并聚类降维，通过改进的特征重要性指标在保持可解释性的同时，在F1-mod上较Multimodal Transformer提升4.3%，较Interpretable Multimodal Routing（IMR）准确率提升3.0%，且重要性分数与人类标注一致性显著更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态情感与行为分类器需要在准确性和可解释性之间取得平衡。当前的Transformer模型虽然预测性能强，但其分布式表示和深度非线性结构使得难以对多模态特征赋予有意义的权重，限制了其在临床情感监测和教育评估等对信任敏感的领域的应用。

Method: 框架包含四个步骤：将每种模态编码为token、提取并聚类概念以降低维度、通过树集成分类器（LDT/LDF/LDAB）路由融合后的模态、使用一种新颖的改进特征重要性指标解释趋势。该改进指标在二分类任务中降低负类影响，从而提升指示器或标记检测的能力。

Result: LDT、LDF和LDAB在F1-mod上较Multimodal Transformer提升4.3%，较IMR在准确率上提升3.0%；所提出的多模态特征重要性在提取显著的跨模态概念时，与人类标注者的一致性显著高于默认特征重要性（IEMOCAP上62.2% vs 43.2%；CMU-MOSI上46.7% vs 32.1%）。

Conclusion: 基于树集成的方法能够在保持可解释性的同时达到与高容量模型相当甚至更优的分类性能，改进的特征重要性指标有效提升了跨模态概念提取的可解释性和人类认可度，为信任敏感的多模态应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Multimodal affect and behaviour classifiers that fuse heterogeneous text, audio, and visual streams must simultaneously achieve competitive accuracy and produce human-understandable explanations of the cues driving their decisions -- a dual objective that current high-capacity models, notably Transformers, only partially address. While Transformers attain strong predictive performance, their distributed representations and deep nonlinearity make it difficult to assign meaningful importance weights to individual multimodal features, limiting their use in trust-sensitive applications such as clinical affect monitoring and educational assessment. We address this gap by developing a framework based on tree-based ensembles that balances accuracy and interpretability. The framework encodes each modality into tokens, extracts and clusters concepts to reduce dimensionality, routes the fused modalities through tree-based ensemble classifiers, and interprets trends using a novel modified feature importance metric. The modified importance reduces the influence of the negative class in binary classification tasks, thereby improving indicator or marker detection. The proposed tree-based ensembles -- Linear Discriminant Tree (LDT), Linear Discriminant Forest (LDF), and Linear Discriminant AdaBoost (LDAB) -- achieve F1-mod gains of 4.3\% over the Multimodal Transformer and accuracy gains of 3.0\% over the primary interpretable multimodal baseline, Interpretable Multimodal Routing (IMR). The proposed multimodal feature importance extracts salient inter-modal concepts with substantially higher human-annotator agreement scores than default feature importance (62.2\% vs.\ 43.2\% on IEMOCAP; 46.7\% vs.\ 32.1\% on CMU-MOSI).

</details>


### [241] [Representation Affects Retrieval: A Case Study of Skill Discovery and Routing in a Multimodal Agent Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20389)
*Kevin Dela Rosa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文以Tinycloud生产级多模态视频代理框架为案例，研究小规模下LLM规划器在上下文中进行技能选择的行为。通过六任务消融实验对比三种暴露模式（全自动加载、默认、全关闭），发现全自动加载能100%正确选择金标技能；全关闭导致执行缓慢和发现失败；而默认模式因词法信号与自动加载的工具技能冲突而导致误路由。核心发现是提示内技能暴露并非单调有益——部分暴露可能因词法竞争抑制正确选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产级代理系统需要从不断增长的技能库中为每个用户任务发现并排序最合适的技能。大规模场景下通常依赖基于嵌入的检索，但小规模下这一选择发生在上下文中（LLM规划器直接在系统提示中评估技能表示）。作者希望系统研究这种小规模内上下文选择行为，将其视为大规模检索式技能路由的小N对应物，以理解技能表示和暴露方式如何影响规划器的选择正确性。

Method: 论文以Tinycloud生产级多模态视频代理框架为案例研究对象，分析其两种技能表示：工具技能（封装单个外部API/系统工具，构成原始词汇表）和工作流技能（编排工具技能调用加模板渲染以生成命名交付物）。技能通过两种系统提示表面暴露：内联主体表面（完整指令、脚本、模板）用于自动加载技能，单行列表用于按需技能。作者设计了六任务选择消融实验，涵盖三种暴露模式：全开（所有技能自动加载）、默认（生产默认配置）和全关（所有技能仅按需单行列出）。

Result: 全自动加载模式下，每个任务都能正确选中最合适的金标技能；全关闭模式导致执行速度显著降低并产生难以克服的发现失败；生产默认配置有一个任务被错误路由，因为该任务的词法信号与一个自动加载的工具技能发生冲突，将规划器的注意力从列表中列出的工作流技能转移开了。

Conclusion: 提示内技能暴露对正确选择并非单调有益：部分暴露会创造词法竞争，从而抑制正确技能的选择。这一小N观察与大规模下基于检索的技能路由研究相联系，论文定位为案例研究而非基准测试，旨在为生产代理的技能表示和暴露策略提供实践洞察。

Abstract: A production agent harness must discover and rank, from a growing library of skills, the one most appropriate for a user's task. At small scale this selection happens in context: the LLM planner chooses among skill representations exposed in its system prompt, without an explicit embedding-based retrieval step. We treat this in-context selection as the small-N counterpart to embedding-based skill retrieval at scale, and present a case study of how Tinycloud, a production multimodal video agent harness, represents its skills for the planner. The harness ships skills under two recurring representations: tool-skills that wrap a single external API or system tool and serve as primitive vocabulary, and workflow-skills that orchestrate tool-skill calls plus a template render to produce one named deliverable. The harness exposes them via two surfaces in the system prompt: an inlined-body surface (full instructions, scripts, templates) for autoloaded skills, and a one-line listing for on-demand skills. A six-task selection ablation across three exposure regimes (all-on, default, all-off) shows that full autoload selects the gold skill on every task; all-off slows execution and produces hard discovery failures; and the production default misroutes one task because its lexical signal collides with an autoloaded tool-skill that pulls planner attention away from a listed workflow-skill. The headline finding is that in-prompt exposure of skills is not monotonically helpful: partial exposure can create lexical competition that suppresses correct selection. We connect this small-N observation to recent retrieval-based skill-routing work at large scale, and frame this contribution as a case study rather than a benchmark.

</details>


### [242] [Nexus: Depth-Adaptive KV-Cache Splicing and Retrieval-Decoupled Tool Routing for Agentic LLMs on Unified Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20397)
*Mustafa Arslan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Nexus系统通过INT8语义查找缓冲区（SLB）将工具路由与schema预填充解耦，使用压缩文本签名（中位数19个token）生成参数，在250个工具规模下路由准确率保持89%，首个参数token出现时间比完整schema重新预填充快1.66倍，节省约80%主上下文token。辅助手段是将编译好的schema KV块移植到活动上下文中，受限于RoPE相位漂移（阈值P=256），采用深度自适应后缀重解码修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCP协议下的Agentic LLM每轮都会重新编码冗长的工具schema，导致预填充成本（与序列长度呈二次关系）在工具注册表增长时主导time-to-first-token（TTFT）延迟。需要解耦路由与schema预填充成本来提升效率。

Method: 1) 主要杠杆：INT8语义查找缓冲区（SLB）+校准的交叉编码器边际门控，通过检索选择工具，参数在压缩文本签名（中位数19 tokens）上生成而非拼接KV缓存，实现深度无关的路由。2) 次要杠杆：将编译的schema KV块直接移植到活动上下文，但受限于RoPE相位漂移，超过阈值P=256时用深度自适应后缀重解码修复，必要时升级为完整重新预填充。

Result: 路由准确率在250个工具规模下保持约89%（基线已溢出上下文窗口）；首个参数token比完整schema重新预填充快1.66倍，节省约80%主上下文token；轻度到中度上下文深度下TTFT加速1.1-1.7倍，深度上下文趋近于持平（可低至0.98x）。两个负面结果：偏离锚点的RoPE保真度边界，以及无参考漂移门预测漂移失败（Spearman rho=0.193）。

Conclusion: Nexus实现"永不退化"（never-regress）属性，保证输出保真度（top-1一致，D_KL≈0），但不保证延迟。所有测量基于单模型元组（Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct Q4_K_M）在Apple-silicon统一内存上，定性边界可泛化，定量范围是元组特异的。

Abstract: Agentic large language models (LLMs) on the Model Context Protocol (MCP) re-encode verbose tool schemas every turn, so prefill - quadratic in sequence length - dominates time-to-first-token (TTFT) as the tool registry grows. Nexus's primary lever is to decouple routing from the schema-prefill cost: an INT8 semantic lookaside buffer (SLB) with a calibrated cross-encoder margin gate selects tools by retrieval, and arguments are generated over a compressed textual signature (median 19 tokens) rather than over spliced key/value (KV) cache. This path is depth-independent: routing accuracy stays near 89% as the registry scales to 250 tools - where a concatenate-all-schemas baseline overflows the context window entirely - and it reaches a first-argument token 1.66x sooner than a full-schema re-prefill at a ~80% main-context token saving. As a secondary, bounded lever we transplant a compiled schema KV block directly into the live context. This is fundamentally limited by rotary position embedding (RoPE) phase drift: an anchored splice is output-exact, but off-anchor placement corrupts attention, so beyond a threshold P=256 Nexus repairs the seam with a depth-adaptive suffix redecode that escalates to a full re-prefill. The resulting never-regress property is a guarantee on output fidelity (top-1 agreement, D_KL approx. 0) - not on latency, which can dip to 0.98x before converging to parity - alongside a 1.1-1.7x TTFT speedup at moderate depth that narrows to parity at deep context. Two negative results bound the design: the off-anchor RoPE fidelity boundary, and the failure of a reference-free drift gate to predict drift (Spearman rho = 0.193). All measurements are from one model tuple (Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct Q4_K_M) on Apple-silicon unified memory; the qualitative boundaries generalize, while the quantitative envelope is tuple-specific.

</details>


### [243] [Environmental Slow AI: Design Principles for Generative Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20398)
*Vanessa Utz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇立场论文（position paper），从环境人文学视角批判当前生成式AI所体现的"最大化主义"价值观，并以此为核心提出"慢AI"（Slow AI）框架下的五项设计原则，将环境可持续性作为AI设计的基础价值，以恢复被无摩擦默认设计悄悄剥夺的人类决策权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI系统在大规模生产文化制品的同时，其设计内部嵌入了特定的文化价值观（即最大化主义价值）。这些价值观一旦被识别，就成为可以被有意重塑的对象。作者希望从环境人文学传统出发，揭示并挑战这种主导性价值观。

Method: 采用立场论文的方法，引入"慢AI"这一已在多个研究和实践项目中流通的概念作为总框架，提出五项设计原则（克制、充足性、选择性优于留存性、物质的可见性、作为可供性的摩擦）。每项原则在两个层面运作：一是设计实施层面，二是在诠释层面，促使使用者与开发者对系统进行反思性投入。

Result: 文章阐述并分别展示了五项原则如何对照当前广泛部署系统的设计，论证这些原则能够通过将诠释性反思融入设计，从而恢复被无摩擦默认设置所悄然消除的人类决策权，延伸人类能动性。

Conclusion: 以环境可持续性为核心价值的"慢AI"设计原则，能够将反思性诠释融入设计本身，修复生成式AI中被最大化主义默认设置所侵蚀的人类决策空间，从而在技术设计中重建人类能动性与环境伦理。

Abstract: Generative AI (genAI) systems produce cultural artefacts at scale, but they also reflect embedded cultural values through their design. Once identified, these values become open to deliberate reshaping. This position paper examines the maximalist values of current generative AI through an environmental humanities tradition and proposes design principles in which environmental sustainability serves as the core value instead. The principles are developed under the umbrella of Slow AI, a term that already circulates across several distinct research and practice programs. Five design principles are articulated (restraint, sufficiency, selectivity over retention, material visibility, and friction as affordance), each of them illustrated against the current design of widely deployed systems. Each principle operates at two levels: a design implementation, and an interpretive layer at which users and developers are prompted toward reflective engagement with the system. Together these principles extend human agency by restoring decisions that frictionless defaults have silently removed and do so by building interpretive reflection into design.

</details>


### [244] [When Retrieval Fails Before It Begins: Structurally Indirect Prerequisite Eviction as a Retention Failure in Agentic Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20400)
*Minkyu Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文挑战了现有检索中心式的记忆管理范式，提出在固定预算下存在一个被忽视的"检索前失败模式"——结构上间接的先决条件驱逐（structurally indirect prerequisite eviction），即与查询弱对齐的上游块在预算压力下被丢弃。作者提供了该失败的操作性定义、可复现的确定性基准和逐种子追踪诊断，并评估了依赖感知的语义垃圾回收（DSGC）——一种一跳图感知规则。在主实验中，DSGC在词法编码器下将全链保留率从0.03提升至0.90，在句子编码器下从0.23提升至1.00。鲁棒性检验进一步界定了该规则有效和退化的预算与规模区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于检索的记忆系统隐含假设必要证据在驱逐后仍然存留，但这一假设在固定预算下并不成立。作者希望揭示并正式定义一种检索前（pre-retrieval）的失败模式——与查询弱对齐的上游先决块在预算压力下被错误驱逐，从而切断链式推理所需的信息通路，并验证一种简单的图感知规则能否缓解此问题。

Method: 作者首先提供了一种结构上间接先决条件驱逐的可操作定义，并构建了可复现的确定性基准和逐种子追踪诊断工具。然后提出DSGC（Dependency-aware Semantic Garbage Collection），一种基于一跳图感知的规则，用于在保留阶段优先保护与下游查询存在依赖关系的上游块。实验在词法编码器和句子编码器两种设置下进行，并辅以鲁棒性检验探讨不同预算和规模区间下的表现。

Result: 在主实验套件中，DSGC在词法编码器下将全链保留率从0.03提升至0.90，在句子编码器下从0.23提升至1.00。鲁棒性检验识别出债务（预算）和规模（scaling）的特定区间，在该区间内一跳规则有效或发生退化。

Conclusion: 作者将"保留先于检索"定位为独立的失败边界，主张系统性记忆不应只关注检索优化，还需在保留阶段考虑依赖结构。DSGC证明了简单的图感知规则即可显著缓解间接先决条件驱逐，同时释放了流水线和失败复盘工具以支持进一步的机制分析。

Abstract: Agentic memory under a fixed budget involves two stages: retention and retrieval. Existing retrieval-centered paradigms implicitly assume necessary evidence survives eviction, but we challenge this by isolating a pre-retrieval failure mode: structurally indirect prerequisite eviction, in which upstream blocks weakly aligned with the query are discarded under budget pressure. We provide an operational definition of this failure, a reproducible deterministic benchmark, and per-seed trace diagnostics. Finally, we evaluate Dependency-aware Semantic Garbage Collection (DSGC), a one-hop graph-aware rule. In our main suite, DSGC improves full-chain retention from 0.03 to 0.90 under a lexical encoder and from 0.23 to 1.00 under a sentence encoder. Robustness checks then identify the budget and scaling regimes where the one-hop rule holds or degrades. Our released pipeline and failure postmortem support mechanistic analysis of retention before retrieval as a distinct failure boundary.

</details>


### [245] [World models of environment, agent and joint agent-environment systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20401)
*Manuel Baltieri,Filippo Torresan,Yivan Zhang,Alexander Boyd,Fernando E. Rosas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文从"建模的通道"而非"预测的变量"这一前提出发，区分了环境通道、智能体通道和联合过程三种世界模型，并利用计算力学为其定义了典范预测模型（ε-转导器/ε-机器），提出了受耦合支持的受限典范模型，证明了环境侧受限状态通过联合因果状态因子分解的核心结构定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型研究通常按预测变量（观测、奖励、状态等）进行分类，但作者指出存在一个更基础的区别——模型所建模的通道。这一区分之前未被系统对待，而它决定了模型所表达的因果结构和预测能力的本质，需要从计算力学的角度给出统一、规范化的刻画。

Method: 文章采用计算力学框架，分别对三种通道（环境通道O|A、智能体通道A|O、联合过程(A,O)）定义典范模型为ε-转导器或ε-机器。随后构建支持受限的环境与智能体模型，其预测等价关系由闭环耦合产生的实际交互延续所限定。核心结构结果通过因子分解定理将受限环境状态与联合因果状态相联系，智能体侧为对偶构造。

Result: 关键结构结果是典范支持受限环境状态通过联合因果状态因子分解，且其转移结构直接从联合模型诱导而来；智能体侧为对偶结果。文章给出了一个POMDP/控制器示例，其中无限制环境模型具有无穷多状态，而由耦合诱导的典范支持受限模型是有限的，展示了支持受限可以极大降低模型复杂度。

Conclusion: 该框架澄清了不同世界模型各自建模的对象，并阐明了耦合与支持受限如何改变其典范预测结构与复杂度，为模型基强化学习中的世界模型选择与设计提供了更精确的理论基础。

Abstract: World models are a central component of model-based reinforcement learning. They are usually discussed in terms of what variables they predict, such as observations, rewards, states, latent or information states. We argue that there is a prior distinction: which channel they model. We consider three cases: the environment channel $O_{:} \mid A_{:}$, the agent channel $A_{:} \mid O_{:}$, and the realised joint process $(A, O)_{:}$, equivalently viewed as a channel with no inputs. Using computational mechanics, we define canonical predictive models for these three cases as $ε$-transducers or $ε$-machines. Canonical environment models recover standard predictive state representations, while the other two give analogous notions of canonical models for the agent and the joint system. We then build canonical support-restricted environment and agent models induced by closed-loop coupling, whose predictive equivalences range over continuations supported by the realised interaction. The key structural result is that canonical support-restricted environment states factor through the canonical joint causal states, and their transition structure is induced directly from the joint model; the agent-side construction is dual. Finally, we give a POMDP/controller example in which the unrestricted environment model has infinitely many states while the canonical support-restricted model induced by the coupling is finite. The framework clarifies what different world models are models of, and how coupling and support restriction can change their canonical predictive structure and complexity.

</details>


### [246] [StateSight: Benchmarking Latent Spatial-State Reconstruction in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20414)
*Michelle Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出StateSight基准测试，包含三个程序化生成的空间推理任务（立方体展开对面推理、遮挡立方体塔计数、4邻域连通分量计数），用于评估视觉语言模型从单张图像重建潜在空间结构的能力。GPT-5.5和Claude Sonnet 5的表现均显著低于人类基线，且格式有效输出掩盖了空间结构恢复失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉语言模型基准测试往往将感知、OCR、领域知识、语言先验和推理能力混杂在一起，难以隔离评估模型从单张图像重建潜在空间结构的能力。作者希望设计一个纯净的、可验证的、避免语言先验干扰的基准，专门考察空间结构重建这一核心能力。

Method: 通过程序化生成构建StateSight基准，包含三个任务族（立方体展开对面推理、遮挡立方体塔计数、4邻域连通分量计数），每个任务族含300个单图像提示，具有确定性真值标签和精确匹配评分。同时引入StateSight-Steps伴生数据集（900个图像-文本交错示例和3600个确定性中间视觉状态）以及可见推导分析，用于追踪错误来源。进行30人人类基线测试（60个条目）作为对照。

Result: GPT-5.5在三个任务上分别达到59.3%、33.3%和28.3%的准确率；Claude Sonnet 5分别达到53.3%、18.7%和7.3%。所有直接运行的最终输出均无格式错误。人类基线（80.8%、68.8%、64.3%）在每个任务上都超过两个模型。可见推导分析识别出图像状态重建和推理过程中的重复性错误模式。

Conclusion: 格式有效的模型响应可能掩盖了对可验证视觉推理所需空间结构恢复的失败。当前SOTA视觉语言模型在隔离的空间推理任务上与人类存在显著差距，表明需要更精细的基准来揭示和提升模型的空间结构重建能力。

Abstract: Vision-language models are increasingly used for multimodal question answering, yet their ability to reconstruct latent spatial structure from a single image remains difficult to isolate. Broad benchmarks often combine perception, optical character recognition, domain knowledge, linguistic priors, and reasoning in the same evaluation. We introduce StateSight, a procedurally generated benchmark for cube-net opposite-face reasoning, occluded cube-tower counting, and 4-neighbor connected-component counting. Each task family contains 300 single-image prompts with deterministic oracle labels and exact-match scoring. OpenAI GPT-5.5, using the API model identifier gpt-5.5, achieved 59.3%, 33.3%, and 28.3% accuracy across the three tasks, while Claude Sonnet 5 achieved 53.3%, 18.7%, and 7.3%. All final direct runs had zero format errors. A 30-participant human baseline on 60 items exceeded both models on every task, with mean accuracies of 80.8%, 68.8%, and 64.3%. Visible-derivation analysis identified recurring errors in image-state reconstruction and reasoning procedure. We also introduce StateSight-Steps, a companion dataset of 900 interleaved image-text examples and 3,600 deterministic intermediate visual states. The results show that format-valid responses can mask failures to recover the spatial structure required for verifiable visual inference.

</details>


### [247] [Categorical AI phenomenology: A first-person approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20420)
*Robert Prentner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种"现象学优先"的人工意识方法，将意识重构为主体通过与世界界面互动所产生的主观体验，并利用基于Q网络的范畴论数学框架来建模第一人称结构与现象学不变量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统人工意识研究多聚焦于第三人称的认知功能或行为指标，缺乏对意识主观体验结构本身的严格刻画。作者希望通过"现象学优先"的立场，将方法论焦点转移到第一人称结构上，为人工意识提供一个原则性的、关系性的理论基础。

Method: 采用范畴论数学工具，从Q网络导出类别来捕捉行动与现象学不变量；将Q网络概念化为编码智能体与世界交互的关系性界面，类比计算机动态状态依赖其感官输入、先前状态和行动的方式。

Result: 构建了一个用于界面意识（interface consciousness）的严格框架，可描述将信息处理嵌入现象学结构的计算系统；该框架与4E认知方法（具身、嵌入、生成、延展）高度一致。

Conclusion: 该论文提供了基于范畴数学的、原则性的、关系性的和现象学的"人工现象学"（artificial phenomenology）理论框架，为计算系统中人工意识的研究开辟了新路径。

Abstract: This paper develops a phenomenology-first approach to artificial consciousness by reframing consciousness as the subjective experience enacted through an agent's interface with the world. We shift the methodological focus to first-person structures, modeled mathematically by categories derived from Q-networks to capture actions and phenomenological invariants. In this framework, Q-networks are conceptualized as relational interfaces encoding agent-world interaction, analogous to how the dynamical states of a computer depend on its sensory inputs, previous states, and actions. Our work provides a rigorous framework for interface consciousness to describe computational systems that embed information-processing into phenomenological structure. The approach aligns with 4E approaches to cognition by emphasizing enactive, embedded, and extended dimensions of experience. The paper thus offers a principled, relational, and phenomenological account of artificial phenomenology grounded in categorical mathematics.

</details>


### [248] [Who Delegates to AI? Evidence from 53,000 Agent Configurations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20425)
*Hyeongjae Lee,Jihyang Cheon,Lanu Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"委托暴露"（delegated exposure）概念，通过Agentic Adoption Index (AAI)衡量职业中工人是否已将任务实际委托给AI代理（即AI工作流中内置了该任务），而非仅衡量AI能做任务的能力。基于约53,000条Manus技能市场中的Agent技能规范和约18,000条O*NET任务陈述的语义相似度，构建职业级指数。研究发现：委托集中的职业与既有人工智能暴露框架识别的职业显著不同；AAI与AI技术能力更接近而非实际使用；AAI在工资分布中部及学士学位层级达到峰值，两端下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI暴露度文献主要衡量AI"能够"执行任务的程度（技术能力维度），但没有度量工人是否"已经"将任务委托给AI（实际采纳维度）。作者认为需要一个新的暴露维度来捕捉真实采纳情况，以更准确地评估AI对劳动力市场的实际影响，避免过高或过低估计。

Method: 首先提出"委托暴露"概念，将AI暴露度从"AI能做什么"扩展到"工人已委托AI做什么"。操作化上，从Manus Skills Marketplace收集约53,000个Agent技能规范（工人实际构建并分享的工作流），将这些技能规范与约18,000个O*NET任务描述分别进行语义嵌入并计算相似度，然后聚合到职业层级，形成Agentic Adoption Index (AAI)。

Result: 三个主要发现：(1) 委托集中度最高的职业与传统AI暴露框架预测的高风险职业存在显著差异；(2) AAI与AI技术可行性（能力）的关联强于与实际使用行为（采纳）的关联；(3) AAI在工资分布偏中部和学士学位层级达到峰值，在最高和最低两端均下降。技术可行性解释了大部分变异，但无法解释最高教育水平职业的缺口，说明可行性本身不能完全解释谁采纳了AI。

Conclusion: 技术可用性不能完全解释AI采纳差异：高教育水平职业中AI委托的不足可能归因于两类原因——工作任务抵抗预先明确规范（难以编码化），或专业人士对工作流程编码速度的自主把控。区分这两种解释并追踪指标随时间的演变需要持续的重复测量。本研究为理解"谁真正采用了AI"提供了操作化测量框架，弥补了能力导向暴露度指标的不足。

Abstract: A growing literature measures how far occupations are exposed to AI, but these measures capture where AI could perform tasks, not whether workers have adopted it. We propose a new layer of exposure, delegated exposure, which records whether a worker has committed a task to AI by building it into a workflow. We operationalize it as the Agentic Adoption Index (AAI), which measures how closely an occupation's tasks match the agentic routines practitioners have already built and shared. We embed roughly 53,000 agent skill specifications from the Manus Skills Marketplace, compute their semantic similarity to about 18,000 O*NET task statements, and aggregate to the occupation level. Three findings follow. First, the occupations where delegation concentrates differ sharply from those pre-AI frameworks identified as most at risk. Second, the AAI tracks what AI could do more closely than what workers currently use it for. Third, the AAI peaks below the top of the wage distribution and at the bachelor's level, declining at both extremes. Technical availability explains most of this variation, but not the shortfall among the most educated occupations, so feasibility alone cannot account for who adopts. That shortfall may reflect work that resists advance specification, or professional discretion over the pace of codification. Distinguishing the two, and tracking how these measures diverge over time, will require repeated measurement.

</details>


### [249] [STCO: Conditional Neural Operators for Time-Dependent PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20477)
*Xingxin Yang,Zhan Zhang,Juan Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种时空条件算子（STCO），用于满足控制/优化场景中时间演化PDE系统在指定条件（如物体运动、入流、力）下的预测需求。STCO通过统一的条件接口将目标时刻条件场注入不同骨干架构，包含流量感知图叶（FAGL）与双站点特征线性调制（DSFiLM）两大组件，在12种骨干架构、3种物理机制和2个预测时间范围上平均降低31.1%的场误差和24.7%的压力推导载荷误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经算子方法对未来状态的预测仅基于观测状态和静态问题描述，但在控制或优化场景中，物体运动、入流或外力等条件是由查询方预先指定的，不能仅由观测状态决定。因此需要能够显式注入指定目标时刻条件场的算子学习方法（PCOL）。

Method: 引入STCO框架，为异构骨干架构提供统一的指定条件注入接口。其条件接口由两部分组成：1）FAGL（非学习的流量感知图叶）：利用最终观测帧的涡量构建固定基数的自适应划分，在区域坐标上共同定位观测历史和目标时刻条件场；2）DSFiLM：在算子计算前后分别通过运动、入流和力三条独立通路，利用当前特征驱动的slot维和channel维门控注入条件信息。

Result: 在浸没边界CFD基准上，涵盖指定运动、入流扰动、体力驱动和形态变化。对12种匹配骨干架构、3种物理机制和2个预测时间范围进行评估，平均配对降低相对L2场误差31.1%、归一化压力推导载荷误差24.7%；11种骨干架构在长时预测上场误差降低；各条件组的消融干预均产生可测量的预测变化。

Conclusion: STCO通过架构无关的条件接口有效解决了PCOL问题，验证了指定条件注入对提升未来状态预测精度的有效性，且对不同的骨干架构和物理场景具有广泛的适用性。

Abstract: Neural operators have emerged as efficient surrogates for time-dependent physical systems governed by partial differential equations (PDEs), but their future-state predictions are often conditioned only on observed states and static problem descriptors. For control or optimization, however, body motion, inflow, or forcing are prescribed for the query without being determined solely by the observed state. We introduce the Spatiotemporal Conditional Operator (STCO) for prescribed-condition operator learning (PCOL), a common interface that supplies prescribed target-time condition fields to heterogeneous backbone architectures while retaining their architecture-specific core computation and context pathways. Its condition interface combines Flow-Aware Graph Leaf (FAGL) with Dual-Site Feature-wise Linear Modulation (DSFiLM). Non-learned FAGL uses vorticity from the final observed frame to construct a fixed-cardinality adaptive partition, then co-locates the observed history and target-time condition fields at its regional coordinates. DSFiLM injects separate motion, inflow, and force routes before and after operator computation through current-feature-driven slot- and channel-wise gates. We evaluate twelve matched backbone architectures with different existing physical and temporal inputs. The immersed-boundary computational fluid dynamics (CFD) benchmark spans prescribed motion, inflow disturbances, body-force actuation, and morphology. Across twelve matched backbones, three regimes, and two lead ranges, STCO yields mean paired reductions of 31.1% in relative-L2 field error and 24.7% in normalized pressure-derived load error. It also lowers longer-lead field error for 11 backbones, while interventions on individual condition groups produce measurable prediction changes for every group evaluated.

</details>


### [250] [Terminal Agents: A Survey of AI Agents in Command-Line Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20485)
*Yi Bin,Xiaoyang Yuan,Haoxi Zeng,Wencheng Ye,Wenqi Shao,Chen Qian,Wei Ye,Yujuan Ding,Zheng Wang,Pengpeng Zeng,Jingkuan Song,Heng Tao Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文档以"终端中介执行"为核心视角，系统梳理了大语言模型智能体在终端环境中的研究现状，提出了七维度的终端能力画像框架，并通过有界固定条件诊断实验揭示了基准家族、系统对比与组件归因等方面的关键发现，为统一研究终端中介智能体提供了框架基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有综述将终端中介行为分散在软件工程、工具使用和计算机使用等多个研究领域中，缺乏统一视角，导致对终端智能体的系统架构、能力习得与评估缺乏整体理解。作者认为终端智能体的核心特征在于其"动作-观察"循环由终端命令执行、文本反馈和有状态环境交互所中介，因此有必要以此作为组织透镜进行系统性梳理。

Method: 本文采用系统性综述方法，以终端中介执行为组织视角，首先界定工作负载层面的研究边界，随后从系统架构、能力习得、评估三个维度进行整合。作为方法论创新，文章提出了七维度的终端能力画像框架（terminal competence profile），并设计了有界固定条件诊断实验，对基准家族的过程信号差异以及配对系统对比中的组件归因局限性进行了实证检验。

Result: 综述发现：终端智能体的实际行为由模型、接口、硬件（harness）、运行时和环境五者共同塑造；可执行的轨迹为基于行动后果、验证与恢复的学习提供了基础，而现有评估偏向最终结果，对过程质量、恢复能力和治理层面的考察不均衡。有界固定条件诊断表明：不同基准家族会暴露不同的过程信号，且匹配系统对比表现出基准依赖的性能差异，组件归因存在局限性。

Conclusion: 研究结论呼吁在终端智能体研究中显式报告系统与运行时条件，并以可重放轨迹和过程级证据作为支撑。所提出的框架为跨软件工程及新兴应用领域统一研究终端中介智能体提供了基础，有望弥补现有研究分散化的不足。

Abstract: Large language model agents increasingly act through terminals, yet existing surveys disperse terminal-mediated behavior across software engineering, tool use, and computer-use research. We regard terminal agents as systems whose dominant progress-bearing action--observation loop is mediated by terminal command execution, textual feedback, and stateful environment interaction. Using terminal-mediated execution as an organizing lens, this survey establishes workload-level boundaries and connects system architecture, competence acquisition, and evaluation through a seven-dimensional terminal competence profile. Our synthesis shows that realized behavior is jointly shaped by the model, interface, harness, runtime, and environment. Executable trajectories ground learning in action consequences, verification, and recovery, whereas prevailing evaluations emphasize final outcomes and expose process quality, recovery, and governance unevenly. Bounded fixed-condition diagnostics illustrate two implications: benchmark families expose different process signals, and matched system comparisons reveal benchmark-dependent performance and limits of component attribution. These findings motivate explicit reporting of system and runtime conditions, supported by replayable traces and process-level evidence. The framework provides a unified basis for studying terminal-mediated agency across software engineering and emerging application domains.

</details>


### [251] [Lost in Translation: How Universal Ethical Values Fail to Translate Across Global Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20490)
*Ozioma C. Oguine,Munachimso B. Oguine,Cesar Cervera,Jenny Yang,Pooja Voladoddi,Mario Rodriguez,Saif Eddin Bani Malhem,Karla Badillo-Urquiola,Daricia Wilkinson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文基于对10个国家14位专家的访谈，揭示AI伦理框架中的价值观（公平、透明、问责）并非普适可操作化的，而是被不同地域的实践者根据当地道德逻辑重新阐释，存在"翻译鸿沟"，并提出了多元化治理的路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI伦理框架将公平、透明、问责等价值观视为普适且可统一操作化的标准，忽视了不同社会文化背景下对AI理解和价值诠释的差异，需要实证研究来揭示理论与实践之间的张力。

Method: 对来自10个国家的14位AI领域专家进行质性访谈，分析他们如何理解AI实践、重新诠释核心伦理价值并构想治理替代方案。

Result: 研究发现AI部署存在结构性不平等条件（基础设施限制、攫取性实践、技术"神秘化"）；专家将隐私重新诠释为集体性和关系性的而非个体性的，将透明解读为信任建构的问责而非技术披露，将公平理解为获取和代表的公平而非结果公平；这些差异被识别为全球框架与本地实践之间的"翻译鸿沟"。

Conclusion: 伦理框架需要从僵化的技术标准转向复数治理（plural governance），重新分配认知权威，并将伦理协商视为持续的、情境敏感的进程而非一劳永逸的标准设定。

Abstract: AI ethics frameworks treat values such as fairness, transparency, and accountability as universal and uniformly operationalizable across contexts. We examined how 14 experts across 10 countries made sense of AI in practice, reinterpreted core values, and envisioned governance alternatives. We found that AI deployment is characterized by structurally unequal conditions, marked by infrastructural constraints, extractive practices, and a "mystification" of technology, which fundamentally shape perceptions of risks and opportunities. Our findings reveal that experts reinterpret values to fit local moral logics: privacy as collective and relational rather than individual; transparency as trust-building accountability rather than technical disclosure; and fairness as equity in access and representation rather than parity in outcomes. We identify these as translation gaps between encoded global frameworks and situated local practices. Finally, we propose pathways toward plural governance that redistributes epistemic authority and treats ethical negotiation as an ongoing, context-sensitive process rather than a settled technical standard.

</details>


### [252] [A Temporal Planning Approach for Intelligent Flood Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20510)
*Fazlul Hasan Siddiqui,Md. Monjurul Islam,Sabah Binte Noor*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于时间规划的智能洪水响应框架，覆盖洪水响应的完整操作生命周期，支持优先级驱动的分诊、路线可达性、资源分配和供应管理，并支持执行中重新规划。框架用ANML和PDDL 2.1两种语言建模以兼容更多时间规划器，实验结果验证了其可行性和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同时应对多个被洪水淹没的区域需要在严重资源约束下按正确的时序协调行动，这是一个具有挑战性的问题。需要一种自动化规划方法来生成时间感知的调度方案，以支持有效的洪水响应决策。

Method: 构建了一个智能洪水响应框架，利用时间规划技术，建模洪水响应的完整操作生命周期。框架包括：优先级驱动的分诊机制、路线可达性与旅行成本计算、资源分配、供应管理，以及应对突发环境变化的中途重新规划能力。框架同时用ANML和PDDL 2.1两种规划建模语言表述，以兼容更广泛的时间规划器。

Result: 实验结果证明了该框架的可行性和可扩展性，表明洪水响应场景能够被有效地建模和求解，同时提供了关于规划器选择的指导建议。

Conclusion: 时间规划技术能够有效支撑洪水响应场景的建模与求解，基于ANML和PDDL 2.1的双语言框架设计提高了与不同时间规划器的兼容性，为实际洪水应急响应提供了自动化决策支持的基础。

Abstract: Effective response to multiple, simultaneously flooded areas requires coordinating appropriate actions in the correct temporal order, under severe resource constraints. Automated planning provides a foundation for addressing this challenge by generating time-aware schedules, given a formal description of available resources, constraints, and goals. This work presents an intelligent flood-response framework that exploits temporal planning and models the complete operational life cycle of flood response. The framework incorporates priority-driven triage, route accessibility and travel costs, resource allocation, and supply management, while also supporting mid-execution re-planning in response to unexpected environmental changes. The framework is formulated both in the Action Notation Modeling Language (ANML) and the Planning Domain Definition Language (PDDL) 2.1, facilitating compatibility with a wider range of temporal planners. Experimental results establish the feasibility and scalability of the proposed framework, showing that flood response scenarios can be effectively modeled and solved using temporal planning, while providing guidance on planner selection.

</details>


### [253] [FL-MAESTRO: Multi-Agent LLM Orchestration for Resource-Constrained Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20518)
*Jiajun Wu,Zirui Wang,Jiayu Zhou,Qiang Ye,Steve Drew*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 FL-MAESTRO，一个由多智能体 LLM 组成的联邦学习运行时编排器，通过三个专业 LLM 代理（分别负责通信拓扑、客户端资源分配和聚合规则）联合做出运行时决策，并由协调器整合和非 LLM 可行性检查确认。实验表明在非 IID CIFAR-10 上，该方法在匹配现有最强能量感知基线精度的同时，将浪费的轮次能量从超过三分之一降至接近零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中通信拓扑是运行时变量，而非固定设计。每轮服务器需同时决策三个耦合问题：通信拓扑、每客户端资源分配和聚合规则。现有工作要么在设置时执行，要么只处理单个运行时维度（如客户端选择），缺乏对三个耦合维度的联合运行时决策能力。

Method: 提出 FL-MAESTRO 多智能体编排器。三个专业 LLM 代理分别对应三个决策维度：通信拓扑、客户端资源分配、聚合规则。一个协调器整合三者的分析形成统一决策，再由非 LLM 可行性检查在执行前确认。编排器消费服务器预测的故障列表，扣留其更新不会被聚合的客户端，消除经典 FL 在易失边缘网络上浪费能量的主要来源；客户端状态以自然语言文本档案读取，无需逐类能量模型即可扩展到异构设备类别。

Result: 在非 IID CIFAR-10 基准上，FL-MAESTRO 匹配最强能量感知基线的精度，同时将浪费的轮次能量从超过三分之一降低到接近零。

Conclusion: FL-MAESTRO 通过 LLM 多智能体实现了对联邦学习三个耦合运行时决策的联合优化，有效降低了易失边缘网络中的能量浪费，并凭借自然语言客户端档案实现了对异构设备的通用支持，代码已开源。

Abstract: In Federated Learning (FL), the communication topology is a runtime variable rather than a fixed design choice, since links and edge devices drop in and out during training.
